**Fourteen chapters, 25,897 words of body prose, day 471 to day 506. Zero bracketed layer lines.** Pressure:
**keeping and being kept** — and nothing else. Full record in `state/continuity.md`.

| **The far-side office that can act** | series | **Vol 5** | **OPEN AND SHARPENED TWICE.** At ch 251 the claim is confirmed standing, entered in good order, travelling on the licence, not affected by the relicensing, and **actionable on this side** — the first office within nine days of road ever in a position to act on it. **At ch 252 the price of acting is named: her own column of hours, and nobody in that room will take it without her word.** The room does not know the figure is hers and has never asked |

THE BATCH'S LAST BEAT.** She said no once at ch 252, in her own head, in about nine words, with no speech and nobody hearing. **A refusal given once can be given again in a different room with a different voice in it.** **The volume's irreversible decision — authorising the office to take the eight hundred jin onto its own book against hours worked past the line — is ch 268, Batch 0003's, and no card in Batch 0001 planted it** |
| **The Hunter's position** | Bai Zhu | **Vol 5** | **OPEN AND NOT WHAT SHE ASSUMED.** At ch 248, at nine feet, in the ordinary voice: **he does not want the claim answered by the far side either**, because an answered claim is an answer somebody above him has to reckon with and he has never believed the reckoning would be the right one — **and the same is true of his own order and he has said that before.** A professional disagreeing with his own office for the second time, on the record, at no cost to himself and at the whole of the cost to her. **He also says he will be on that side of the post for the rest of the run and will not come inside nine feet again unless she puts him there.** **His request is still pending** |
| **The light in the tower, and nine men on a floor** | Feng / crew | **Vol 5, by report only** | **OPEN, AND IT IS NOW KNOWN ONLY BY REPORT.** At ch 244 a hauler at a pot gave three facts she could not have got any other way and did not write down: **a tower with a light on at the fourth hour that does not stop; a bundle going down a stair and a clerk's arms going up four flights and coming down empty; and a floor of nine men and the same nine and all of them still on it.** **Feng does not appear. No scene west of the line. No document arrives.** The fair copy of the Foundation column going past the line into a book the far side keeps is **ch 262, Batch 0002, and is unspent** |
| **The far-side board on the post** | series | **Vol 5–6** | **OPEN, NOT ANSWERED, AND NOT RE-COUNTED.** Its image was ch 236 and ch 238 and **re-counting it is the procedure loop.** What is on the page in this batch is the routine only: a strip goes up every nine or ten days whether or not there is anything to put in it, and the strip that went up this month is one she will never be able to read |
| **The road west** | series | **Vol 5, Batch 0002** | **SHUT FOR THE SEASON AND IT GETS ITS OPENING DAY IN BATCH 0002.** Named once in this batch, at ch 243, by Wei Ran, who has no date for it either. **Wei Ran quoted the going at ninety coppers for a person and one bag. The coming is a different number and nobody has quoted it** |

### Held, confirmed, not touched by Batch 0001

- **The claim stands at eight hundred jin and is not answered, not paid, not assessed and not torn off. Hou
  Sanliang is unpaid, patient and never met. The date in his counting room in Lowwater is untouched. Luo Ping
  is a direction. No draft has been written and none has been sent — Batch 0002's.**
- **The Hunter's request is still pending.** The space holds the countersignature dated from the far side and
  that is not an answer. **The line's own people still do not know she exists, and the second line's people
  know her hours and not her name.**
- **The relicensed licence was never opened. The register is never mentioned. The Clay Plate is still worn
  against her ribs and was not described. MEMBER ABSENT stands three times and was not raised. What became of
  the eleven is not raised. The ch 150 refusal is not softened. The year of non-forgiveness is not mentioned at
  all in these fourteen chapters and is still carrying. The three sponsors' second book is lodged, copied and
  spoken nowhere. Ning Wu does not appear. No second Lung and no second appointment.**
- **The office above this county is not named.** He read the Standing Office of the Second Account in one
  breath at ch 236 and stopped, and **nothing in ch 239–252 goes one notch further and nothing in it even
  tries.**
- **The surface answer is NOT paid.** Batch 0001 pays the narrower thing only: *a figure is only paid against
  something, and the only thing in this country that can be charged for is a day somebody worked.* The volume's
  surface answer is **ch 267**, in a worker's mouth, in short sentences, and **no card in Batch 0002 may pay it
  early.**
- **The right index finger is not named, priced or foreshadowed.** Four fingers' worth of grip left on that hand
  and used at ch 242. Nothing gives it back and nothing offers to.
- **Power: Foundation 9 for all fourteen chapters. Golden Core 1 is ch 258, day 512, Batch 0002.** Merit zero,
  the channel not restored and not measured, Ledger not ordered and not refused.

### Still open at the close of Volume 5, recorded by the Volume 5 close and handed to Volume 6

- **The Hunter's request is still pending.** The space holds a date, *Held*, and the countersignature on her
  petition in an office's hand; none of it is an answer. A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. **The
  line's own people still do not know she exists.**
- **Everything above the Standing Office of the Second Account is still unread.** Nothing in ch 239–298 goes
  one notch further and nothing in it tries. **No Scribe, no Anchor, no Sisterhood, nothing about what he was
  before he died.**
- ***Kiln-born*** is in two heads and on no paper. Zero across Volume 5.
- **What became of the eleven** is not raised anywhere in Volume 5.
- **The register still reads *reconciled***, read as the subject of a chapter at ch 282 and again at ch 298,
  never corrected. **The Clay Plate is still worn against her ribs**, described once and never taken off.
- **The year of non-forgiveness is still running**, touched at ch 297 at low volume and retired nowhere.
  **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.**
- **No second Lung and no second appointment.** Feng never on the page in Volume 5 after ch 262.
- **Wei Ran appears four times in Volume 5, at ch 243, 253, 269 and 290** *(corrected at the volume close —
  this file previously recorded twice)*, every time as a rate and a road and an opinion. Nothing a romance.
- **She has no wages, no season and no booking at the close of Volume 5.** The column closed on the thirtieth
  in another man's hand, eighteen counterfoils in four marks and no nineteenth, and the three working days
  before it were never paid and never will be.
- **She is entered as good in a far-side office's book and cannot be un-entered.**
- **Yuan is readable from outside the bound without her, and therefore findable** — and the chapter that made
  him findable said on the page that being read means being sent for. **He has lost the date of the morning
  he died. The Ledger is no longer hers to order and there is nothing she can order instead.**
- **What is past the last working point**, farmed and lit, with offices above the one office whose name is
  known — she stood half a mile short of a mill whose wheel has not turned since before she came, and went no
  further. **This is Volume 6's question and it is on the page as a fact, not as a plan.**
- **Carried as proposed repairs for a phase that may reopen closed chapters, not applied here:** the straight
  ASCII apostrophe sweep across Volumes 1–4 (**1,588 instances**; Volume 5 is clean) and the named calendar
  months still standing in closed Volumes 2 and 3 (**fifteen instances: February, March, June, September,
  December**). Both are character-level, both are mechanical, and both belong to a phase already doing a canon
  sweep across volumes. **Neither may be done as a side effect of drafting.**

## Volume 4 — every thread below is confirmed OPEN on this file. Nothing here is retired.

The close audit added **eleven items that had to be preserved after Volume 3**, and all eleven are open
and are carried. They are listed as a single block under *What the Volume 3 close audit preserved, and
what Volume 4 therefore owns* below the live-table, with the thread each one belongs to. **Retire
nothing in that block before the thread's own volume.**

| Thread | Owner | Planned resolution | State |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **The instrument not held in this county, and the empty answer space** | Bai Zhu / Yuan | **Vol 4 — the engine** | **OPEN. THE SPACE TOOK ITS FIRST ENTRY AT ch 210 AND IT IS NOT AN ANSWER.** A field agent of a Warden's office **cannot unmake a System inside a bound without a Wall writ**, said once, in his own mouth, in a low room in front of twenty people, at ch 174. He certified in a Warden's hand and **referred the dismantling upward**. The request for the instrument went live on the same crate. **The district has answered: received, numbered, referred upward, no instrument held in the district either, and an empty space the width of a line where an answer was meant to go** (ch 176). **At ch 210 a carrier came back east on the third of a month with the county's round and a slip of county paper folded into the same bundle, and the space has a date in it and one office word: *Held*. It means the paper is in a room and nothing has been done to it, and that is all it means.** **She heard a second meaning in it, she is the only person within nine days of road who could, and she told nobody and did not put it into a sentence with a verb even in her own head.** **DO NOT ANSWER IT IN BATCH 0004 AS AN ANSWER.** Batch 0004 puts **the countersignature on her petition** into the space, in an office's hand, dated from the far side, **and that is not an answer to the request.** The request stays pending to the last page of the volume: a Wall writ is not issued to a field agent, because a Wall writ is issued by the line's own people to somebody who lives on it, **and the line's own people closed their ground against her and still do not know she exists.** At ch 196 he walked east ahead of her, bought nothing, asked nothing, and said the same thing about himself in the same breath; at ch 215 he told her in the ordinary voice, in front of her, **that he does not want his own order executed and wants the answer pending**, and at ch 218 the line's own people executed it anyway because the paper says so and they are right |
| **The referral** | series | Vol 4–6 | **OPEN AND NOW EXECUTABLE.** Gone up the stair past every office she has ever stood in front of. **Carries her name, her stages in order and her hours. Carries no agent's name. Signed by the office and not by a person.** The dismantling it orders **can only be executed past a boundary no county paper crosses** — and **since ch 198 her hours have been in a contractor's book, in order, with dates, past that boundary, and she put them there with her own mouth.** Staying inside used to keep her out of its reach and under a ceiling of nine; **she paid for the countersignature with the other half of that pair.** Both halves were live at ch 192 and the execution half is now the live one |
| **The Foundation petition — barred, not refused, not withdrawable** | Ning Yan | **Vol 4 — ANSWERED IN LEG THREE, ch 266** | **NO LONGER OPEN AS A QUESTION. The answer came at ch 266 on day 266 from an office past the county's boundary, in an office's hand and not a person's, on a schedule read aloud out of a contractor's wage-book by Ren Shu of the schedule office at Stony Ford, whom she was not shown the wedge of paper for. Foundation 1 was reached on day 276 and announced at ch 207, heard by nobody; Foundation 5 was reached on day 360.** What the answer cost is on the page: a document she never touches, a number she cannot check, four weeks of a body that has never been full and cold at the same time, five days of a fire she did not put into, a count of breath that does not come back, and the order out of a left hand. **Recorded as:** Received, numbered, routed, barred on the ground that **this county holds no schedule of work done past its boundary and cannot certify one**, expressly not on the standing of the applicant, with a last line written slower than the rest: *the answer to this application is not this office's to write.* **Once entered it cannot be withdrawn by her.** **The countersignature on this petition is what goes into the district's empty answer space at the volume's last pages, and that is not an answer to the Hunter's request** |
| **The Wall** | series | **Vol 4 — DESCRIBED AT ch 199, ON THE GROUND, NINETY PARTS TRUE** | **NO LONGER UNDESCRIBED.** At ch 199 a foreman of about fifty put his hand flat on a contractor's book and said the word: **the line.** Not stone, never in the same place on both sides of itself, a line of **work**. The work is **tending**; the job is **the course**; where the work is done and done well and on time the line holds; where it is not, it leaks, and what comes out of a badly kept stretch is **drift**, thicker where the work is worst. The tender's own account of the drift: what is said inside goes in the ground on the far side and runs along and pools where the ground is soft. **He says that is ninety parts and that he has never seen the tenth.** What it is made of and who put it there is **Volume 6–10 and is not answered here.** Nothing on either side of it was described in the batch. In Volume 3 it existed only as **a writ this county does not hold**, said once at ch 174, with **no description of anything on either side of it.** `bible/world.md`: no one inside the mortal world thinks of it as a wall; it is **not known as an object until Volume 4**. **Do not describe it, cross it, name the office above this county, or put geography past a marker post before Volume 4's own logic has earned it.** The **Warden's marker post near the Broken Kiln** (ch 18) is a local ward line and **is not the Wall** — do not conflate them |
| **Wei Ran, slow-burn line** | relationship | begins Vol 4 | **OPEN AND SHE HAS NOW APPEARED, ONCE.** She is **twenty, mortal-born**, a carter with **one team out past the far end of the working reach and back**, and **the first person Ning Yan has met who went through without permission.** **Named at ch 221 by the sub-contractor, appeared at ch 222**, answered one direct question in four words and did not elaborate, and **said the correct thing about the line as a fact and not as comfort** — the drift is thin out here because nobody works it, and it is thin here and not thick up there for a reason that has nothing to do with the ground. **She goes off page at the end of ch 222. The line is off page and is not the main relationship. Nothing in Volume 4 is a romance and the book does not romanticise that Ning Yan is eighteen** |
| **The year of non-forgiveness** | Ning Yan / Yuan | runs from ch 100; changes shape in Vol 4 | **OPEN AND NOT RETIRED. IT CHANGED SHAPE IN BATCH 0001 AND DID NOT CLOSE, AND IT WAS NOT TOUCHED AT ALL IN BATCH 0003.** From ch 100 through all of Volume 3 and into Volume 4. **At ch 185 she spent the once-a-year reserved sentence — the one she kept back to say out loud that she had not forgiven him — on the Ledger price instead, so that when the year came round at ch 188 there was nothing set aside for it. At ch 188 she did not say it, and what replaced the performance is a purchase: she goes on using him on purpose. She records that what she feels is relief at not having to prove to an empty room that she is angry, and immediately that the relief's cost is that she has just agreed to spend him.** At ch 206, in Batch 0002, it was touched **once, at low volume**, as a purchase that still carries. **Fourteen chapters in Batch 0003 do not mention it.** **She does not forgive him and does not tell him to stop.** `outline/ending.md` is fixed that she forgives him in ch 1000 and **it is housekeeping, not a relief, and the account closes because he is gone** — so **the account must still be carrying at the end of the book and no volume may retire it** |

The date was not torn off at ch 177 and was not torn off at ch 187; she has not been to the counting room; the claim is not resolved; Hou is unpaid, patient and never met.** **Do not resolve it and do not reopen the ch 140 body** |
| **Hou Sanliang, of Lowwater** | Ning Yan | Vol 4–5 | **OPEN.** Unpaid, patient, **never in a room with her, never met**, through all of Volume 3 **and through all of Volume 4 Batch 0001.** **His counting room is in Lowwater, where she is standing** — two Volume 3 chapters wrongly called it nine counties away and both were repaired at the audit. The ch 150 handover would have paid him in full out of a purse that is not this county's and **she refused it in a room with two witnesses.** **She stood in a lane nine minutes from his counting room's door at ch 188 and did not go in. His room holds a date, and Batch 0001 now holds the name of the hand that wrote it (Luo Ping). The claim is not resolved and Hou is not met.** |

**The clock:** day 0 = the last day of the thaw. Ch 132 = day 39 · ch 148 = about day 130 · ch 163 = about
day 184 · ch 178 = day 219 · **ch 179 = day 220 · ch 192 = day 253**. The season's road **shut on day 117
and reopens on day 254**, per a sealed county notice on the public shelf by the door — *or later, and not
earlier* — and the thaw is on about the same day and **was eleven days off at day 243**. **Months are thirty
days and a month begins every thirty days, so month-day = `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` and day 134 was the first of
a month** (ch 150: day 136 is the third of the month; ch 222: day 376 is the third of a month, as it must be
eight months on). **CORRECTED AT THE VOLUME 4 CLOSE: this block used to read *days 374 to 463 read
day − 373*, which was wrong for days 404 onward. The correct form is the formula above. Equivalently: days
374–403 read day − 373 · days 404–433 read day − 403 · days 434–463 read day − 433 · days 464 onward read
day − 463.** The monthly attendances are
**day 147 the fourteenth, day 178 the fifteenth, day 209 the sixteenth, day 239 the sixteenth** — thirty
days from the third, the drift ending. Volume 3 ran **day 2 to day 219**; **Volume 4 Batch 0001 ran day 220
to day 253**; **Batch 0002 ran day 254 to day 280**; **Batch 0003 ran day 281 to day 376**, and ch 223 onward
is Batch 0004. **In Batch 0003 the attendances fell on day 299, day 329 and day 359 — all three the
sixteenth, thirty days apart — and day 284, day 314 and day 344 were the firsts of three months.** **The four
stages in Batch 0003 fell on day 296, day 316, day 338 and day 360, at intervals of twenty, twenty,
twenty-two and twenty-two days.** Batch 0004's first pinned day is **386 (Foundation 6 at ch 224)**, the
thirteenth of the month that began on day 374, and **Batch 0004's attendance is day 389, the sixteenth, thirty
days on from day 359 and the last one before the volume closes.** Batch 0004 is **sixteen** chapters, ch 223–238.
**Volume 4 as a whole runs day 220 to day 470 and closes at day 470, the seventh of a month, with her across
the line and entered in a wage-book on the far side.**

Eleven men and a girl of nineteen have an ordinary working word for it — *a stretch that has got loud* — and they say it as a fact about ground. **She kept what the board says against what nine counties have heard, in order, over four years, and is the only person alive who can: the loud stretches are nearest the inside end, the worst is the worst-worked, and the quietest ground on nine days is at the far end, which is where there is nothing to announce because there is nobody there.** She said it to herself once, in one plain sentence, and **told nobody, including him.** **It cannot be proved — the only control in the world is herself — and nothing in this batch or this volume proves the crews wrong or supplies the tenth part.** **There is no word within nine days of road for it, and there is no word for the quiet either** (ch 222). **Batch 0004 owns what she does with it** |

The reason was **the Hunter's own referral, read out of his notebook at a table by him**, saying the thing in her head is unauthorised and is carried by a licensee in good standing. It reached her in nine words at a trestle at about the sixth hour and **she said nothing.** **The consequences are open and they are Batch 0004's standing condition: the stretch went back in three days, the run is shorter, the contract is smaller, the board has a hole in it, nine men are idle, and the girl of nineteen is idle and she is somebody's daughter** |

  column her hours go in and she wrote nothing, and Ma Sheng did not offer the word a second time.**

- **The claim stands** with its date in a counting room in Lowwater. **Hou Sanliang is unpaid, patient, never
  met, and does not appear in ch 209–222.** **The ch 140 body was not reopened.**
- **The referral and the request are still pending** at the last page of the batch. **The space is no longer
  empty but it has a date and *Held* in it, and that is not an answer.**
- **The office above this county is not named.** **Yuan is silent about it throughout ch 209–222**, which is
  the correct use of him here. **He reads it at ch 236 and not one page before.**

### The one structural question — the protagonist — ANSWERED AT THE VOLUME 4 CLOSE

**Yuan has been a voice only and off-panel for two volumes of sixteen.** `outline/series.md` §2 names
**Yuan, male**, as the protagonist and `state/book-metadata.md` records **Gender: Male**, so the male-led
requirement is met at series level. **Volume 4's close third is on Ning Yan throughout.** Yuan is present
through ch 179–238 as a voice: present-tense speech, rationed to four marks and then to one, a standing held
twice and refused once, and a name read at ch 236 that he had said four times he could not read.

**ANSWERED. The finding, stated without softening: sixty chapters of close third on a woman who is not the
protagonist, in which the protagonist has no scene, no body, no room and nobody to speak to, is a structural
debt two volumes of sixteen old. It is not enough.**

**What he did across ch 179–238, all of it his own decisions, all of it paid for by her:** he **found a hand
that wrote a date against a claim** (eight days out of ten, ch 187) · he **held a standing for a crossing and
was paid for it out of a base that was not finished** (one day out of three, stated at ch 231, taken at ch
233, put down when the crossing was over) · he **intercepted a share of a settled job and spent it** (ch 235,
out of her foundation, one stage short of finished) · he **restored his own life** (about six weeks at the
last page) · and he **read one name he had said four times he could not read, in one breath, and stopped**
(ch 236).

**What Volume 5 does about it, decided before Volume 5 opens:** **the shape of Volume 5 is itself the
correction, inside its own sixty chapters.** Volume 5 must put Yuan on the page in his own right for **at least
one sustained scene in which he acts, or is acted on, away from her hearing** — not more rationed sentences,
not a description of him, not a dream — **and it must take something that is his and that the ending still
needs**, because `outline/ending.md` is fixed that he refuses the bargain, takes the fall and is gone before
ch 1000, **and a man the reader has never seen in a room cannot carry a fall.**

**What may not happen:** the close **may not** re-frame ch 179–238, **may not** re-title the volume, **may
not** introduce a new final enemy, and **may not** take anything Volume 5 owns — **the family account, Ning
Wu's decade, the four months erased in Chapter 3, and the bargain and the fall. The Scribe is fixed in
`outline/ending.md` and belongs to Volume 15**, and this decision does not touch it.

**The full statement is in `state/current.md` under *The protagonist, answered in writing*, and in
`workspace/volume-04-close/SUMMARY.md`.**

### One unspent item the close audit added

**Feng's fair hand copying the Foundation column, and his copies going up a stair that now belongs to a
district clerk and past the line into a book the far side keeps.** `outline/volume-04.md` promised this *from
ch 224*. **It is not on the page: Feng appears once in the whole volume, at ch 181, on day 223, before
Foundation 1 at day 276, and Volume 4's Batch 0003 and Batch 0004 cards forbade him appearing without a card.**
What is on the page and confirmed: alive, warm for the first time in twenty-two years, sixty-two, the fair
hand, the district clerk carrying the bundle up four flights with a strap over his shoulder and coming down
with his arms empty, a quarter off his own stair at his own written request entered not refused and not
thanked for, the Listener's standing for life, **and that she did not thank him** (ch 181). **No second
appointment and no second Lung is spent — Volume 11 owns them. Column at five is carried canon and is not
restated in Volume 4.** **The beat is available and it belongs to the far side, which is where Volume 5
starts.**

### Two rules the close review made binding, one of them now canon

1. **NO NAMED WEEKDAY, EVER.** **The world has no named weeks.** A day is an hour of the day and a day of a
   month, a month is thirty days, month-day = `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` with day 134 the first of a month, and
   characters say *four days up and four days down*, *the third of the month*, *the second hour of the
   afternoon*. **The rule is now written into `bible/world.md`** under *Rules of the world that hold
   everywhere* and again under *What must never appear*. It was inferred from Volume 4's practice, enforced
   retroactively at one line, and broken eight times in closed Volumes 1 and 2; **all eight were repaired at the
   review** (ch 40, 47, 104, 109, 111 ×2, 114) and a grep of `chapters/` for all seven weekday names returns
   zero across ch 1–238. **Volume 5 may not name one either, from narration or from any character's mouth.**
   **Two closed batch cards still describe beats in weekday terms — `outline/batches/volume-01-batch-0003.md` and
   `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0002.md` — and neither beat is on the page. Do not lift either phrase.**
2. **ABSOLUTES IN THIS BOOK ARE LITERAL.** A sentence that says *every part of her that had gone wrong*, or
   *never used as a metronome*, or *the volume's only instance*, is a claim about the whole manuscript and will
   be checked against the whole manuscript. Two of the close audit's own records broke this rule and were
   corrected in place. **Write the claim you can hold.**

### The volume-close phase is required and has been written

`workspace/volume-04-close/PROMPT.md` was written in the same call as ch 238, because ch 238 is the last
chapter of Volume 4. **It audits ch 179–238 against `outline/volume-04.md` line by line, walks the fifteen
*Required continuity facts to preserve after the volume* one at a time with the three changed-shape answers
stated plainly, answers the protagonist question above, and creates exactly one follow-up prompt — the Volume
5 planning phase. **The close has since been reviewed. Its findings are in `state/current.md` under *Close
review repair* inside the `## Volume 4 close audit — CLOSED` block, and in `workspace/volume-04-close/SUMMARY.md`.
Three of them change what a reader of this file must know: two sentences moved in ch 220 and ch 232, the
weekday rule above is now canon and six closed chapters in Volumes 1 and 2 were reopened and repaired under it,
and the audited figures are 120,572 over the volume with Batch 0003 at 28,581. One finding is not the writing
phase's to make and is recorded for the controller: `workspace/volume-04-close/` carries no `.done` marker.**

## Volume 3 — the two structural questions

**Both decided** in `outline/volume-03.md` (*The relicensing*), **both on the page since ch 132, and both
spent**: route
through the sponsors; **clearance (ch 130) → three sponsors' seal (131) → Sun He's countersignature
(132)**, the order inverted from Volume 2 on purpose; **ceiling at Condensing 9 in her own hand**;
**seven conditions with the claim re-entered last**. The gathered reasoning behind the decision is kept
under `## Volume 3 — the two open structural questions` in `state/current.md` for the record. Read the
outline for the plan, not that section.

**The Ledger break is ch 149 and is SPENT.** She broke her own ch 118 term in public, on her word, with the
price named before it — **four and a half weeks out of about ten** — and the four sheets of the handover
came back verified true line by line. **He put it down the same day and refuses permanently for the
volume. From ch 150 Ledger is hers to order and his to hold only on her word.** **Batch 0004 touches no
price and no Ledger.**

## What the Volume 3 close audit preserved, and what Volume 4 therefore owns

The close audit confirmed **eleven items had to be preserved after Volume 3. All eleven are confirmed
on the page** (`state/current.md`, audit row 10). Each one is a live thread belonging to the row above.
**None may be retired before its own volume.** Do not treat this block as a to-do list; it is the reason
several rows above are still open.

1. **Condensing 9, announced at her own order; the ceiling lifted 5 → 9 by relicensing on her own hand;
   Foundation petitioned and barred.** *Belongs to: the licence, the referral, the petition.*
2. **The relicensed licence with all three legs, seven conditions, and the claim last.** *The licence.*
   **Read once at ch 132 and never re-listed.**
3. **The claim standing, Hou unpaid and never met.** *The claim.*
4. **The three dead crew, causes entered.** Du Hong (day 121, Volume 2), Cao Lei and Xu An (day 21,
   Volume 2). **Neither crew name is used again; Du Hong's is used four times in Volume 3 as remembered
   weight inside live scenes and that is the settled record.** *The burial line — closed.*
5. **Feng's Lung taken; the quarter off his stair; every hour upward true; column at five; alive.**
   *Feng Lushi.* **A second appointment or a second Lung is Volume 11 and is unspent.**
6. **The two items on nobody's paper, and nothing answered about the eleven.** *kiln-born*, the office
   name, the eleven.*
7. **The register still reads *reconciled*.** *The register.*
8. **The plate still worn.** *The Clay Plate.*
9. **Ledger broken once on her public word, refused again the same day, then hers to order, about ten
   days left.** *Yuan.*
10. **The year of non-forgiveness running.** *The year.*
11. **Bai Zhu first named at ch 150, on the entered refusal, and never before; the handover paper signed
    by the office and carrying no agent's name; the referral of the dismantling upward for want of a Wall
    writ.** *Bai Zhu, the referral, the instrument.*

## Promises to the reader, by chapter

- **Ch 1 (under 800 words):** done. **Ch 3:** done. **Ch 12:** done. **Ch 24:** done. **Ch 31:** done. **Ch 36:** done. **Ch 42:** done.

## Deliberately unexplained, for the whole run

The fourth child of the Ning household. Whether the Scribe is sincere. What the Anchor does when it is released. Whether anything answers from beyond. Whether Ning Wu forgives her. What the eleven Chosen Ones before her became. Who reads Yuan's line. **Who wrote the twelve unsigned sheets in the frame at the back of the quota office — two of them in Captain Lu Tao's roundhouse hand, the other nine in four hands nobody has claimed — and what the other eleven say.**

## Do not do these

- Do not introduce a new final enemy. The Scribe is the last one and is fixed in `outline/ending.md`.
- Do not resolve the central mystery before Volume 11.
- Do not end the book early. The planned end is chapter 1000, volumes 1–16.
- Do not make the wall's existence the work's mystery — the mystery is *why it is there*, and the reader gets the honest partial answer around Volume 6.
- Do not use *the shape of a happy life* again before Volume 16.
- Do not restate, for effect, any of the spent constructions listed at the foot of `state/current.md`.
- **Do not open a second accident, frostbite, starvation, death or rescue arc.** **Batch 0009's pressure was grief and a licence; Volume 3 Batch 0001's was a wage, a lane, a column and a man with a calendar. Batch 0002's is audit and mercy.**
- **Do not soften the refusal.** She has named it in two documents and in public. Nothing in Volume 2 undoes it.
- **Do not put outline language in a character's head or a narrator's line** — *this chapter, this volume, the next chapter, in this batch, the whole series, the reader*. It leaked four times in batch 0008 and twice more in closed Volume 1; all fixed. Sweep for it every batch.
- **Do not record a chapter-length trade-off.** Floor 1,300 words; a batch under it, or declining monotonically across its chapters, is a failed batch. Add scene material inside the cards, never pad.
- **Do not reopen the Watch, the burial line or the county debt line.** All three are lodged and closed on the page; see `state/current.md` canon changes 18–20.
- **Do not re-list the seven relicensing conditions (read once, at ch 132) or re-explain what a surety is for, the suppression arithmetic, the position-not-characters rule, the name in the wrong order, or the head count in the crew quarters.** All are fixed in `state/continuity.md` and all were audited once at the Volume 2 close. **The crew is nine men and her — ten sleepers, twelve bunks — and 104–118 was repaired to say so; do not write it as ten men.** Use them; do not teach them.
- **Do not spend a second strike request or a second outside-bound office.** The slip at ch 110 is the only one in this run.
- **Do not lift the ceiling without relicensing, and do not relicense without a signature.** Both happened, once, at **ch 132** — clearance (130), three sponsors' seal (131), Sun He's countersignature — and the ceiling is **Condensing 9** with seven conditions and the claim last. **Nothing further is licensed without another relicensing, and the second needs a schedule the county does not keep past the boundary; that is Volume 4.** **Both structural questions are now decided, in `outline/volume-03.md` (*The relicensing*)** — route through the sponsors, signature all three plus Shen Xiu plus Sun He, no leg skipped and no signature forged or borrowed. Read the outline; do not re-derive them. The leg order is clearance → seal → countersignature, **inverted from Volume 2 on purpose**; do not tidy it back.
- **Do not let her forgive him before the opening of Volume 4.** The year runs from ch 100.
- **Bai Zhu is named, and the naming is SPENT.** He is unnamed through ch 149 and **named once at ch 150**, by the agent himself, to a clerk taking the name of the agent who laid the paper, because a form requires it. **The handover paper carries no agent's name. He has never given his name to anybody he chose, and he is now on a Warden's book and in the county book forever. Do not re-name him on a document he does not sign and do not have anybody else speak the name as a revelation.**
- **The ch 133–148 and ch 149–163 prohibitions are spent and held.** The arm beat is Voice at no cost and must never be written as a cost. **No bare Settlement fraction goes on the page in Volume 3 — weeks only**, and the channel is named twice in Batch 0003 (about ten and the price at ch 149; about three at ch 160) and **not again in Batch 0004.**
- **Do not break the silence bought at ch 128.** Nothing about the sponsors' second book — the nine struck households, the eleven-year spacing, the fourth line citing the maintaining members' ledger, the roundhouse hand in the frame of twelve unsigned sheets — is lodged, copied or spoken anywhere in ch 119–178. Check every chapter.
- **The audit and the Lung are spent.** Audit ch 136–140, offer ch 141, **taking ch 143**. Feng is a **Listener**, alive, coughing less, column at five, suspended from the stair for one quarter and not dismissed. **He must not be offered a second appointment and he must not take a second Lung before Volume 11.**

## Volume 3 Batch 0004 (ch 164–178) — cost and signature — CLOSED

**The record of what Batch 0004 was given, kept so a Volume 4 drafter can see what has already been spent.
Every one of these is on the page and none of it may be re-run.**

- **Condensing 9, ANNOUNCED at her own order on day 198, in the open, witnessed by nine men at the cold
  end.** The column reads **one, two, three, five, six, seven, nine** — three holes in nine lines — and
  nine counties have it in order. **The cost is on the page**: nine counties told exactly where she stands
  a fortnight before the referral goes up the stair, and **the cold end fills with people and stays filled
  and is never hers again.** Nothing here may be announced a second time.
- **Ceiling nine in her own hand**, relicensed ch 132, **seven conditions, the claim the seventh — never
  re-listed in the batch and referenced twice.** **There is nothing above nine on the front of the document
  anywhere in the world**, and a registrar has said so out loud in a room. **The second licence is nailed
  STRAIGHT above the second bunk; the first is rolled in the lining of her coat.**
- **Hunted in writing.** The refusal stands in a Warden's book; the county examines her monthly, not
  quarterly, at the third hour, in person, in her own hand — **the third attendance was day 209, the
  sixteenth**. **The heel of her left hand is gone and she told nine men so out loud at ch 172.**
- **About ten days of channel** against about six weeks of winter still to come in it — said once, at
  ch 178, in weeks, in the final image, and nowhere else. **Ledger is hers to order and his to hold only on
  her word, and at ch 178 she has it to order and does not order it. No price is named anywhere in
  ch 164–178 and no Merit exists.**
- **The winter return was read in order at the second hour on day 212, in public, with a queue out into
  the square**: one, two, three, five, six, seven, nine, three gaps ruled in the ordinary hand with four
  words, *Member present* at the foot, the ruling spoken aloud and **stopped** because he has said it three
  times already, and **her own signature in eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger.**
- **The Hunter certifies in a Warden's hand that the System is real, unauthorised and carried by a licensee
  in good standing, and refers the dismantling upward to the Warden for want of a Wall writ.** The referral
  carries her name, her stages **in order** and her hours, **carries no agent's name**, and has gone up
  past every office she has ever stood in front of. **The request for the instrument went live with it.**
  The district has answered *referred upward, answer pending*.
- **The Foundation petition is filed, numbered, received, routed and BARRED — not refused — and cannot be
  withdrawn by her.** Only an office above this county can complete it, **and only past the same boundary
  the dismantling needs.**
- **The board behind a well has eight frames on it, two of which had come off and are back**, and Da Shan
  now charges nine coppers a load and says so to her face. **The tin is about a hundred and forty coppers.**
- **The word *irregular* is on the front of the Qinghe furnace schedule for the fourth time this year**,
  and nine men have read a nine, a bar and three holes, and **Gao has done the sum out loud in a hall so
  nine men have heard it from a mouth instead of off a wall.**
- **The girl of twenty-four is alive**, paid a quarter to the thaw in advance, and now has four coppers a
  week more out of a district fund for the cold months, because she wrote the second document and not the
  first. **Hou Sanliang is unpaid, patient, and has never been met.** Shen Xiu, the three sponsors and
  Wei Ran do not appear. **Nothing about the sponsors' second book is lodged, copied or spoken anywhere
  in ch 119–178.** **She does not forgive him, and she does not tell him to stop.**
- **Not resolved, and deliberately so:** what became of the eleven · the office Yuan cannot read · the
  design of the Foundation tax · whether Ning Wu forgives her · the Foundation writ, dead and not revoked ·
  **the dated entry against the claim** · the name his own office writes above this county ·
  **the Foundation itself, which is Volume 4's.**

## Do not do these — Batch 0004 additions (all held; the batch is closed)

- **Do not soften, defer, reopen or regret the ch 150 refusal.** It is entered in a Warden's book, her
  member's sheet, and the county book. Nothing in 164–178 can unwrite it and no chapter may let her
  wonder what she would have done.
- **Do not take the referral before ch 169 and do not spend it at all before ch 175.** What is spent at
  163 and only at 163 is **a request on a public shelf for an instrument this county does not hold.**
- **Do not put the ninth stage in the column by accident.** The winter return reads one, two, three, five,
  six, seven, **nine** — the eight is a space and always is.
- **Do not re-run the suppression arithmetic again.** It is spent three times: ch 104, ch 159–160
  and ch 161. At the reading, Sun He rules the gaps and stops, as he has ruled them three times already.
  **The signed-shift roster rule is spent with it** — stated once, by Cao Lei at ch 60, demonstrated at
  ch 65, 66, 71 and 72, and not to be explained again.
- **Do not meet Hou Sanliang and do not answer the dated entry.** A date against a claim is a schedule,
  and Batch 0004's job was the reading and the referral, not the claim. **Carried into Volume 4 under the
  close-audit additions at the foot of this file — stated there once, not twice.**

## Do not do these — the Volume 3 close audit additions (all held; the volume is closed and audited)

These come out of the audit itself and bind **Volume 4** first.

- **Do not use the phrase "eleven weeks" again.** It is retired. It carried **at least five** different
  meanings across fourteen chapters of Volume 3 — the Hunter's span in this county, weeks of the lane,
  weeks of savings, weeks of winter remaining, and a forward projection — and was arithmetically wrong
  against the day table in most of them. **If a Volume 4 chapter needs the Hunter's span in this county,
  re-derive it from day 2 (his arrival, ch 119) to the day in question. If it needs the winter, the winter
  ends at about day 261** (ch 163, day 184 plus eleven weeks; ch 178, day 219 plus six weeks).
- **Do not let "about four seconds" become a metronome.** Thirty-six uses across twenty-six chapters of
  Volume 3. It is a rhythm and not a rule, and Volume 3 never restates the four seconds or the release rule
  from ch 53–54 — **Volume 4 must not either**. If a pause needs a number, use a different number.
- **Do not put the claim's counting room outside this county.** Hou Sanliang's counting room is **in
  Lowwater**, and it is where the dated entry was made (ch 155). Two Volume 3 chapters wrongly called it
  *nine counties away*; both were repaired at the audit.
- **Do not let the footprint of four counties and the footprint of nine counties blur.** **This is Volume 1
  canon and it runs on hearing versus paper, not on stage column versus claim.**
  **NINE counties = what is heard.** An announcement is audible inside the bound and every county takes its
  own copy in its own time in the words of whoever reads it out — ch 46 (*the same advancement, in four
  counties of nine*), ch 55 (*going round nine counties for three days*), ch 114, ch 171.
  **FOUR counties = what is written.** The county books a stage column is entered in, the licence that is
  hung on a furnace schedule, the annex, the claim, the four county books that each billed Ning Bo twice,
  and the Bureau's quarterly bundles — ch 53, ch 55, ch 58 (*the design was on a document in four
  counties*), ch 132, ch 138, ch 140, ch 148, ch 159, ch 160, ch 163, ch 176.
  **A third thing that is neither:** ch 132 says a certified copy is *readable in order by anybody in nine
  counties with a stamp and a reason*. That is **obtainability**, not the location of a book, and it is
  what makes her visible and expensive. Ch 178's *nine counties that had it in order* is the **reading**,
  not the books. **The Volume 3 close audit got this rule backwards and three chapters were reverted at
  the close review** — see `state/current.md`, *Close review repair*.
- **Do not soften the referral, and do not answer the empty space.** It is gone up the stair past every
  office she has ever stood in front of, and the district has answered *referred upward, answer pending*.
  **Volume 4 opens on that answer space.** Do not fill it in the first batch.
- **Do not describe the Wall, cross it, name it, or put geography past a marker post on the page.** In
  Volume 3 it exists only as **a writ this county does not hold**, said once, in Bai Zhu's mouth at ch 174.
  Nothing else about it was spent.
- **Do not name the office above this county.** Yuan has now said four times, in front of her and on the
  page at ch 178, that he cannot read it and is not going to say it a fifth time to be different about it.
- **Do not retire the non-forgiveness early.** It runs from ch 100 into the opening of Volume 4. She has not
  forgiven him for the price of the ch 149 reading and does not thank him for it, both said once, in that
  order, at ch 150, and restated at ch 157, ch 163 and ch 176.
- **Do not meet Hou Sanliang, answer the dated entry, or resolve the claim inside Volume 4's first batch.**
  The ch 150 handover would have answered it in full and she refused it in a room with two witnesses, and
  nothing in 151–178 softened that. **This is the one place the rule is stated; the Batch 0004 block above
  points here rather than repeating it.**

She is carrying it folded in three against her ribs. **Nothing above the copying floor has been entered, described or named. No card in this batch may refuse it and no card may end a chapter on a plan to refuse it** |
| **Yuan addressed as a holder** | Yuan | **Vol 6, ch 338 — spent** | **DONE AND PAID.** Out of her foundation, told before spent, so that the ring in the shed at the mill holds without her standing there. **He is no longer nowhere an office can address, and being held means being sent for — and he said so himself in the ordinary voice: being sent for is a thing that happens to men in rooms with doors.** The date of the morning he died is still gone and ch 272's finding is not softened. **Refusing a second holder is ch 355, Batch 0004's** |
| **The box circuit** | series | **Vol 6, ch 344 — broken** | **BROKEN, and said in a room with a person.** A book that shuts on the thirtieth has a beginning and an end on everything in it; the materials account against the mill and the holding under her days have neither. Four words came back down the stair: **It goes as it is.** The tray went up with a page fanned open in it, a strip came off it, and a keeper who has kept that board nine years pasted it in because that is what the board is for. **A question came back down the mill road on the tenth day — the first thing in nine years to come down it.** Nobody refused and nobody broke anything: **she cleared some work** |
| **The cross-reference** | series | **Vol 6–7** | **NEW AND LIVE.** Every month a man with a brush on the copying floor copies her column out against another book by hand, *so that a thing about you is also a thing about what you have done.* **She cannot see either book and neither can see the other**, and the fine hand is the one holding his. The room above the counter reads what goes up the road against everything else that went up it |
| **Her body at the last page** | Ning Yan | **Vol 6, GC8 at ch 352 and GC9 at ch 357** | **GOLDEN CORE 7. Eight of eleven holds** (nine at the ninth of a month, eleven at the start of Volume 6). **Five counts of breath on a loaded reach and never four there.** Four fingers and a thumb, right arm open half the time, aperture cracked permanent. **A third of a reach at a gate and one and a half coppers a day, down from half and two and a half at GC5.** **Four listed things cleared in seven weeks, four lines left on the board, five more coming down over two months, and then the yard goes quiet until the season turns** |
| **The Hunter's request** | Bai Zhu | **Still open at the last page of Volume 6** | **ON THE PAGE, ch 335 only, and it is still pending.** He bought nothing, asked nothing, and said once in the ordinary voice that **he does not want the sending answered either**, because an answered thing goes up and somebody above him has to reckon with the answer and he has never believed the reckoning would be the right one. **A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. The line's own people still do not know she exists.** He also said *your brother is on it* once, flatly |
| **The structural answer** | series | **Vol 6, begun — CLOSED AND AUDITED** | **BEGUN IN SEVEN MOUTHS, IN SHORT SENTENCES: ch 331 (the man with the brush), 335 (the Hunter), 337 (the listing man), 346 (the man behind the stack), 350 (the man who keeps the list), 353 (the carter) and 357 (the man with the brush again, at the finding).** The line out west is kept by men, paid by the day, families on it, and **nobody east of here has ever needed one thing out of it** until she asked for her name on a wall. **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong. The origin answer (Volume 11) and the moral answer (Volumes 15–16) are nowhere previewed.** *(Corrected at the close audit: the record previously said FOUR mouths in Batch 0003 at ch 331, 335, 337 **and 345**. Ch 345's mouth is the keeper with the paste pot and it says nothing about the line west. There are seven, not eight.)* |
| **The fine hand** | series | **Vol 6–7** | **NEW AND OPEN.** She can read her own name on the strip that came down and cannot read four or five lines in the hand that has a holding under her days. The keeper cannot read it either. **Somebody is going to have to stand in a building with a counter in it and a floor over the counter and answer a question, and the answer is not hers to read first** |

| **The reach** | **NEW. Begun once, by accident, on the twenty-second, on a bank past the mill.** Does not come back. She tried it on the twenty-fourth and the twenty-fifth and it does not repeat, and the not being disappointed was the other half of the cost. **An office wrote it down afterward in a fine hand she cannot read, at a gate post, three days later, and she does not need to read a word of it because she knows the date** |
| **The refusal** | **Entered and standing.** *Ning Yan, of Lowwater. The holder is not presented. Refused at the counter. The twenty-sixth.* In the counter's own seasonal book, in a building she cannot reach, **and no hand in that building can take it out** |
| **Her own book** | **Standing on a stone under a keeper's ladder at a post on the mill road, nine feet off a road, open in the middle, unreadable to the only man holding it.** **No office that reads that fine hand is ever going to know where it is** |
| **The second vouching** | **Copied upward out of the office that keeps it, in a hand she has never seen, by somebody she will never meet, and sent back down her own road so a man with a brush could set her against herself.** She cannot be un-entered twice over |
| **The closed-season box circuit** | **BROKEN and billed.** No book on that reach shuts clean. Four trays went up with a page fanned open and a fifth came back down. The road now runs both ways |

| **The Hunter's request** | **STILL PENDING.** On the page at ch 335 and ch 347. **A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. The line's own people still do not know she exists** |
| **The fine hand** | **Open.** She has now seen its output three times — the holding (ch 336, 357), the upward copy of her own vouching (ch 357), and the reach (ch 360) — and has never once been able to read a letter of it. **She knows what is in it every time and cannot read the hand** |
| **Everything above the copying floor** | **Unread and unnamed.** No office is named anywhere in ch 299–360, and nothing above the copying floor, nothing above the second office, and nothing above the Standing Office of the Second Account is entered, described or named |

this block has happened yet: this is what the volume is planned to open, close and leave open, so that the first

Volume 7 is what happens when the rooms that read her are larger than any room she has stood in |
| **Being sent for (his condition since ch 338)** | Yuan | **Vol 7, ch 401–403 — opened on the page, paid on the page** | **OPEN AND DELIBERATELY UNRESOLVED AT THE VOLUME 6 CLOSE.** Volume 6 neither resolved it nor had it happen off-page. **Volume 7 opens it at ch 401 and answers it at ch 402: the holding is used on purpose to move an office, and the answer comes down her road as a question about her.** Not foreshadowed in his mouth before then, and not resolved off-page |
| **The fine hand** | series | **Vol 7, ch 417 — the volume's refusal** | **OPEN.** She has seen its output three times (ch 336, 357, 360) and has never read a letter of it. **At ch 417 a man whose job it is offers to read it to her once, on the ordinary condition that she confirms or denies it, and she refuses, because a denial is an entry. She goes on not knowing, forever, on purpose** |
| **The cross-reference** | series | **Vol 6–7, running** | **LIVE.** Every month a man with a brush copies her column out against another book by hand. He copies down and has never in eleven years copied a thing upward and is not the man who could |

He is on the page exactly twice in Volume 7, ch 361–374 and ch 405–420, nine feet off, notebook shut, buying nothing, and **she asks him both times**. A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent and the line's own people still do not know she exists |
| **The structural answer** | series | **Vol 7, continued — six mouths, not paid** | **BEGUN IN SEVEN MOUTHS.** Volume 7 continues it at ch 365, 379, 391, 398, 409 and 415, in six new mouths, four or five sentences each, never as a speech. **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong** |

### Threads the plan opens, and the pages they are pinned to

| Thread | Pinned at | What it is |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **The first thing that has ever gone east** | ch 365 | From ch 358 the offices read her where she stands, and the first thing they do with a reading they cannot send for is read it out of the room. A strip goes east on the end-of-month cart and **does not come back on the cart that takes the empty trays up**, and nobody on that ground has ever sent anything east. **This is the thread Volume 8 inherits** |
| **The rooms have learned to look** | ch 371 | A stage used to reach a strip three days late, in a fine hand, at a gate post. **From ch 371 it happens in daylight in front of two or three people on that ground**, and she is entered as a body and not only as a column. **This is the mechanism of the volume's central pressure and it is the reason she is the weakest thing in every room** |
| **A stage cannot be built by one pair of hands** | ch 371 | NS 2's cost: the furnace will not light itself and she cannot light it alone. **It is the engine of the whole volume — she must be with somebody every time her furnace lights, which is the reason she ends up in rooms** |
| **Her hand stops being able to lie** | ch 383 | NS 3's cost. She can no longer enter a false line in any book on that ground, and there is no hand she can pay to enter one for her |
| **A thing she reaches cannot be put back** | ch 394 | NS 4's cost, and it is paid in public on the lifting floor at ch 419–420, where the floor is a block short for a week |
| **She cannot be given anything** | ch 404 | NS 5's cost, and its first bill is a cup of water handed to her across a bench in ch 405, with no romance in it anywhere and with her doing the refusing |
| **She cannot be paid** | ch 412 | NS 6's cost. The bare board was a season; this is the rest of the run. **It closes the pay question for the rest of the book and it is not the same thing as Volume 6's work without hire** |
| **She can never again refuse a reading** | ch 402 | The cost of the break, paid out of her and not asked for. A second refusal would be a refusal of an answer already given, and an office holding an answer already given does not accept a second one. **Nothing takes the refusal at ch 358 out of its book** |
| **The third frame scene** | ch 401 | Away from her hearing, in his own frame. He is **answerable** — a third thing, worse than being sendable, because an answer can be quoted. **His unentered state, his unnamed state and the date of the morning he died all stay gone** |
| **What she was taken for** | ch 390, paid at ch 420 | **The midpoint is the reversal and the volume's last line is its answer.** She was taken for her column, and a column's one talent is that it can refuse, and nobody on that floor has ever had one to read. At ch 420 she reads a column out loud in the biggest room she has stood in, and it is the only thing she can do that has cost her nothing but time |

### Held, confirmed, and not touched by the Volume 7 plan

- The register still reads *reconciled* and **Volume 7's allowance for reading it is 0**. The Clay Plate is worn and
  **not described**. MEMBER ABSENT stands three times and **may only be carried, never raised as a surprise**. The
  relicensed licence is **never opened**, and no card may say whether it would need relicensing.
- **The year of non-forgiveness is still running** and **Volume 7's allowance for mentioning it is 0** — as in
  Volume 6, where the subject stood not once in sixty-two chapters.
- **Ledger returns zero across Volume 6 and Volume 7's allowance is 0.** Hand is spent once in Volume 7 (ch 402) and
  refused once (ch 419), and neither is in Batch 0001. Merit settles three times and **nothing in Volume 7 kills
  anybody.**
- **No office is named in Volume 6 and Volume 7 names none.** Nothing above the copying floor, nothing above the
  second office, and nothing above the Standing Office of the Second Account is entered, described or named.
- **No harem and nothing a romance.** Wei Ran has **at most two cards in Volume 7, one in Batch 0002 and one in
  Batch 0004**, and is a trade, a team, a rate and an opinion and nothing else. **Ning Yan never says her mother's
  name.**
- **Feng does not appear in Volume 7 at all** — no scene, no stair, no hand, no quarter — and no second Lung and no
  second appointment, both of which are Volume 11's.
- The claim is ANSWERED and not reopened. Hou Sanliang is paid, never met, and not on the page. Ning Bo's account is
  discharged where it can be closed and **Ning Wu's decade is a line with no mark on it, and neither is on the page
  in Volume 7 and no card may put either on it.**
- **The surface answer is paid once at ch 267 and is not re-voiced.** The origin answer is Volume 11's and the moral
  answer is Volumes 15–16's, and **no card in Volume 7 may preview either.**
- **The Order of the Recruited is a Volume 8 antagonist and not one word of it is on Volume 7's page.**

### Where the Volume 7 close will find the run

**If Volume 7 delivers what the plan says it delivers:** she is at **Nascent Soul 7** with a furnace that will not
light itself, a hand that cannot write a false line, four fingers and a thumb on one hand, an arm open half the
time, eight of eleven holds, a licence she has never opened, **no way ever to be paid and no way ever to be given
anything**, a refusal standing in a book she cannot reach and **a second refusal standing nowhere at all**, and a
column she has read out loud in a room full of people who could break her with a hand. **And the strip that went
east did not come back.**

**That is the position Volume 8 opens on, and it is stated here so the volume close has something to walk against
rather than a hope.**

| **The two lines** | **NEW AND OPEN.** The column nine days of road east, in the fine hand, has **two lines at the foot of it where it had four for four months.** A man with a brush saw the shape of it on the sixth and told her on the tenth and would not guess at it. **The change came about three days after the strip went east and she cannot read a word of either shape.** It is the first thing in six months that has come out of one of those rooms toward her, and it came back as less |

No money, no board, no name of hers anywhere in the yard.** The sill line is going east and the yard is on the road the strips go down |

### The batch's own reversals, paid on the page

- **The board filled on the eleventh of a month (day 864) and came back with eleven dressed-stone lines priced by the
  ton, and she can take none of them, and the only work left narrows to reading, which nobody on that ground will pay
  for.**
- **Her hands went cold on the second of a month and have not been warm since.** They had been warm for four months.
- **A yard prices a body, and every office she has been entered in has her column in it, and there is nowhere on nine
  days of road that has her arm on a line of her own.** That is the volume's pressure, said once, at ch 368.
- **She had been the thing in every room she had walked into for six months and had been getting it wrong.** A room is
  where a thing is put so that four people can agree about it later, and the thing has been her name since the
  twenty-sixth of a month about six months back.

- `Ledger` returns **zero** across all fourteen files. **Hand is not spent in this batch** — it is spent once in Volume 7

  else.**
- **The Hunter's request is still pending. A writ is not issued to a field agent. The line's own people still do not
  know she exists.** He is on the page once, at ch 369, nine feet off, notebook shut, buying nothing, asking nothing,
  and she asked him. **Nothing about his paper, his order or his brother is raised.**
- **No office is named anywhere in ch 361–374 and nothing above the copying floor, and nothing above the second office,

### What Batch 0002 inherits, in one paragraph

**Day 899, the sixteenth of the month that began on day 884. A gate three miles east of a mill whose wheel has not
turned, a shed a mile and a quarter west of that, and a yard four miles east at the turn.** Nascent Soul 2, taken on
the third of a month in the open in front of two men, and every stage from here is a public fact on this ground. A
furnace that will not light itself and cannot be lit alone, cold hands, one copper a day at three and a half feet,
bread five days in seven, four barrow-loads of clay against a wall and a fifth of a sack in a barrow, a full board
she cannot take a line off, and a length three days in seven at a yard that pays her in a cup of something hot. Four
fingers' grip on one hand, the right arm open half the time, eight of eleven holds, five counts of breath on a loaded
reach, the bar needing two hands, the licence never opened, the plate worn and undescribed. A strip that went east and
did not come back, and a column that came back with two lines off it. He is readable, findable, held and sendable and
nothing of that is opened.

Batch 0002 (ch 375–389), measured by grep, not promised***.**

### New in this batch, and open

| Thread | Status at ch 389 |
| --- | --- |
| **Her name is in a book on that ground** | **NEW, AND IT IS THE BATCH'S IRREVERSIBLE STEP.** At the fourth hour on the twenty-eighth of the month beginning 914 she said a woman's name out loud in a room, asked her for four hours a month, and **gave her own name to get it.** Rao wrote *Ning Yan, of Lowwater* in the ordinary hand in the stair book she has kept for sixteen years — who went up the nine steps, on what day, at what hour. **It goes up the mill road on the first at the sixth hour with a cord round the tray, comes back down on the ninth, and is copied out into a book on the floor below by a man with a brush who cannot read the fine hand and has never been able to.** **She cannot read it and there is nobody inside nine days of road who can read it back to her. There is no hand in that building that can take it out.** **This is the first thing she has ever put into a room on purpose that she cannot get out of again, and she got it by asking for it out loud** |
| **The furnace is not a place** | **NEW, AND IT IS THE ENGINE OF THE VOLUME.** It comes up in whatever room she is standing in if there is another living thing in that room, and it comes up under the nearest person who puts a hand on the ground. She cannot choose the room and cannot stop it being seen, and **the ring in the shed stands at a finger's width and has done for forty days.** From ch 378 **she must be with somebody every time her furnace lights, which is the reason she ends up in rooms** |
| **A man with a burn on his hand** | **NEW, AND CLOSED AS A SOURCE.** About fifty-five, nine years on a board at a post two miles up the mill road, a paste pot and a rag, a woman and a boy. **He came for his strips on the first of a month and put his left hand flat on the floor of the counting house and she said *yes* when he asked her.** He has said he is not going to think about it again and he did not ask her for anything. **She is not going to ask him.** He is unnamed and stays unnamed and **he is not available** |
| **The third floor, and Rao** | **NEW, AND THE OPEN ROOM.** Nine steps and a landing with a turn in it, a table the length of nine men, forty books open at once, cold at the north end. **Rao has kept columns there for sixteen years, has read forty thousand, holds no furnace and cannot lift, and is the smallest person in the biggest room she has been in.** **There is no line on that floor with Ning Yan on it and Rao has said so and is right. A margin correction is four coppers. The stair book is the only thing in that room that is not about work.** **Rao's structural answer is ch 391 and is Batch 0003's, and it is unspent** |
| **A false line cannot get into a book on that ground** | **NEW, AND KNOWN TO ONE PERSON.** From the eleventh of the month beginning 914 **there is no hand, hers or anybody's she can pay, that can enter a line that is not true into any book inside nine days of road.** Before it there were three places at four coppers. **Nobody on that ground knows it except her, and she cannot tell anybody without telling them the other thing** |
| **The arm** | **NEW, AND PERMANENT.** Four fingers and a thumb on the right hand and **she cannot close it.** It went into the other thing for two seconds at the fifth hour on the twenty-first of the month beginning 914 when a four-foot ditch came in on her, and it has not come back. **A closed fist was never in the eight of eleven holds** |

### The batch's own reversals, paid on the page

- **A listed ditch line at the north end of the yard at the turn, four hundred yards by the length, four feet, off a
  works board between the twentieth and the twenty-second of a month** — the first time a man with a rule in his hand has
  asked her for anything, and he asks her a day early and says he is telling her now so that it is not a thing that
  happened to her tomorrow. **Rubbed off his own board with his own thumb, and no name of hers anywhere in that yard.**
- **A furnace that comes up under a man’s hand because she was in the room, and she said *yes* out loud and did not
  say the four words she had.**
- **Her hand stops being able to lie**, and the bill for the only thing she ever wanted to buy arrives inside a week of
  it and she can neither pay it nor take it.
- **She has four hours a month of a warm room because she asked for it out loud, and the first thing she gave in
  exchange was her own name in a book she cannot read.**

### What Batch 0003 inherits, in one paragraph

**Day 950, the seventh of a month that began on day 944. A gate three miles east of a mill whose wheel has not turned,
a shed a mile and a quarter west of that, a yard four miles east at the turn, a counting house on the mill road, and a
third floor above it with nine steps and a turn in the landing.** Nascent Soul 3, taken on the eleventh of the month
beginning 914 at the seventh hour in a room with the door shut, **bracket 2 of six**, and her hand cannot lie. **A
furnace that lights only where she is with another living thing in the room.** A book on the top floor with her name in
it going up the mill road on the first. Fifteen coppers and no bread. Four fingers and a thumb and an arm that cannot
close. A strip that went east and did not come back, and a column that came back with two lines off it, and a man at a
gate two miles up the mill road with a burn on his hand, and a woman of forty-five who has read forty thousand columns
and who is going to read something out loud in a room.

Volume 7 Batch 0003 (ch 390–404), measured by grep, not promised***.**

### New in this batch, and open

| Thread | Status at ch 404 |
| --- | --- |
| **She can never refuse a reading** | **NEW, AND IT IS THE BATCH'S IRREVERSIBLE COST, AND IT CANNOT BE SPENT BACK.** On the twenty-second at about the eighth hour a man behind a counter read her a question off a strip in daylight on a step and she said *Yes*, in the ordinary voice, at four feet, and he wrote eleven words in the seasonal book, which shuts: *Ning Yan, of Lowwater. At the gate three miles east of the mill. The twenty-second.* **The reason is not a rule. An office on this ground now holds an answer that has already been given, and a second refusal would be a refusal of an answer already given, and an office that holds an answer already given does not accept a second one.** **She cannot know, for certain, how far that goes.** |
| **The reach** | **NEW, AND IT IS THE ENGINE.** It goes four feet and stops, it does not go where she is looking, and **a thing she reaches cannot be put back where it was.** Nine deliberate uses in nine days at the yard at the turn, **five dressed stones out of the low side of that yard**, one of which she put there trying to undo an earlier one. **She has found out that the fire moves when she asks**, and that is the same fire as her furnace, and nobody inside nine days of road can be told |
| **A line read upward** | **NEW, AND IT IS THE BREAK.** At ch 401, away from her hearing, a man who is held lets a send reach him and answers it, and the answer is carried up the holding to the place that sends strips down the mill road, **which has never been done on that ground in eleven hundred years of practice.** It arrives on her road on the twenty-second as a question and is answered on a step. **She was not got, and she worked that out on the boards nine times while standing in the middle of a made road** |
| **Yuan is answerable** | **NEW, AND IT IS WORSE THAN BEING SENDABLE.** He can be findable and refused once. **An answer cannot be refused at all, because an answer is the only one of the three that is a thing anybody can quote.** Said in his own mouth in the third frame scene at ch 401. **His unentered state, his unnamed state and the date of the morning he died all stay gone and none of them is given back.** `Ledger` returns **zero** across all fifteen files and **no figure of what is left of him is on any page** |
| **Her name in two books** | **NEW, AND IT CANNOT BE TAKEN OUT.** At ch 403 she asked a woman of forty-five, out loud, in a room with forty books open on it, to take her name out of the stair book. **She cannot get it out of that one and she cannot get it out of the copy on the floor below, because both books went off this floor for eight days and came back.** Rao said it in four sentences and was right about all four. **This is the first time since ch 388 that she has asked anything out loud and been told no, and it was not a refusal, and she cannot tell which of the two she is holding** |
| **Two holders against a date** | **NEW, AND IT IS GOING UP A ROAD.** The yard book of a yard at the turn came back down the mill road twice with two lines at the foot of the page. The second time it said **two holders are now required against the seventeenth of a month and there is one holder on this ground.** The man with a rule in his hand wrote his margin note in his own hand, exactly as he said he would eleven days before, **and three men at that yard have read it** |

### The batch's own reversals, paid on the page

- **A woman who has read forty thousand columns read one back to the person it belongs to, and the column contains a
  refusal, and it is the only refusal in the set** — and the strongest thing in that building is a column, said once,
  in Rao's mouth, and not said again.
- **A stage in the open at the seventh hour with nine men on a yard**, and then a reach put into a stone on purpose
  with a man's leg under it, and the stone went four feet sideways into a hole.
- **She tried to put it back and it opened a second hole**, and four men on the bed said *they have put another one in
  there* without turning round.
- **Merit settled once on a listed sill course, the share told twice before it was spent, and the fire went off for
  four days** and came back on the sixth, on its own.
- **A strip came into a counting house with no cart and no post on the road**, and the answer that came with it was
  about a woman at a gate, and she said yes.
