## Volume 7 Batch 0004 (ch 405–420) — what this batch added, and what is live going into Volume 8

**Volume 7 is complete on the page at ch 420. This is the last batch of the volume and it closed the volume's own
question in a room. Everything below is confirmed OPEN at ch 420 and nothing here is retired.**

**ADDED AND LIVE GOING INTO VOLUME 8**

- **The fine hand, and what it says about her. SPENT AT ch 417 AND NOT GIVEN BACK.** Dai read it four times on the
  night of the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,034. He offered to read it to her once, in a room, in daylight,
  on the ordinary condition that she confirm or deny it. **She said no.** A denial is an entry, so her no went up the
  same road every answer has gone up since before the wheel on that mill was cast, and there is a name at the head
  of one of four sheets in a groove in a room with a lamp in it. **Nothing in that building failed. Nothing was
  written. No book will not shut.** She is poorer for it for the rest of the book. **Do not reopen this. Do not let
  any card say it is reconsidered, softened, queried, or mentioned in passing by anybody. It stands.**
- **The bill for the refusal.** The floor's only response is to treat her as somebody who may know something, four
  times a month, forever, with nothing written down anywhere. **She would rather be entered**, and was asked about it
  at ch 418 in her own hearing, and cannot un-ask it.
- **A question on the third floor with nothing at the other end of the rule**, asked on the twenty-ninth of the
  month beginning 1,034: *is there anybody inside nine days of road who could read the hand it is in.* She did not
  answer it. The man behind the counter shut the book.
- **What a send costs the person it is sent for, which the Hunter gave her on the made road in the ordinary voice:
  six months.** Not the road and not a room. It needs a line with her name at the head of it and a rule under it,
  and where she is standing is not a part of it. **Her line already exists and her no is already on it.** He does not
  write anything she says down.
- **The board at the works is full and cannot pay her and is not going to.** Thirty-four lines on the fourth,
  forty-one on the seventh, forty-nine on the third of the next month, every one measured by the weight or the
  length. **The pay question is closed for the whole of Volume 8 and does not turn again.**
- **A second column on a lifting-floor sheet that has been empty for nineteen years**, filled in on the thirteenth of
  the month beginning 1,064, in Geng's own hand, by a man of about forty, because a woman of eighteen read the sheet
  out loud and stopped him in the middle of it. **A floor that runs a second column is a floor that will be asked to
  run it again.**
- **A floor a block short**, a dressed stone four feet off its frame that is not going back, and a bell that has
  already gone.
- **The reach will not go out of a room she has not been asked into.** It went four feet when a man of thirty asked
  her for it, in a room of nine men, in front of all of them, and it had not gone once in two hours when there was
  nothing in the room that needed it. **She is a thing that has to be pointed by somebody.**
- **Two things of hers are in books that shut and neither is a line, a column or a person**: four words in chalk in a
  yard-book hand, and a slip with a date and a place on it written by Rao in a hand that is not hers. **Both cost
  nothing and neither pays her. The man with the brush has asked to see the slip three times and said yes the third
  time.**
- **Four sheets of her going up the mill road once a month in a bundle with a cord round it**, already written in a
  hand the man with the brush cannot read and does not want to, and he has never seen the day before the second and
  does not know where on nine days of road they are written.
- **The man who reads the fine hand knows four people have been in his room in twenty-six years**, and one of them
  was a woman in her situation nine years ago who said a thing was right when she knew it was not, and is alive.
- **Geng's list.** A piece of paper, a name on it, not his hand, not a room he has ever been in, nineteen years and
  never taken off it, nothing in that building about it, and he has stopped thinking about it **on purpose, because
  the day he starts again he will get it wrong.**

**CARRIED AND STILL OPEN, unchanged by this batch**

- **The Hunter's request is pending.** A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. **The line's own people still do
  not know she exists.**
- **Everything above the offices that vouched is unread and unnamed, and no office has been named at any point in
  sixty chapters.**
- ***Kiln-born* is in two heads and on no paper.**
- **What became of the eleven.**
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. Read and never corrected, and not read at all in this batch.**
- **The Clay Plate is worn against her ribs and has not been described. Volume 7 did not take it off and did not
  describe it.**
- **The year of non-forgiveness is still running and the subject was not mentioned once in ch 405–420.**
- **The relicensed licence still says Foundation the first stage on the front of it and she has still never opened
  it.**
- **The strip that went east on the end-of-month cart and did not come back on the cart that goes up with the empty
  trays**, and the column that came back with two lines off the bottom of it, and a stair book going up a road once
  a month with a name in it in two books that shut.
- **Her own book is standing on a stone under a keeper's ladder at a post nine miles west, and she did not go to it
  in Volume 7.**
- **Hou Sanliang is paid and has never been met. Ning Bo's account is discharged where it can be closed. Ning Wu's
  decade is a line with no mark on it. None of the three is on the page in Volume 7.**
- **Yuan's unentered state, his unnamed state and the date of the morning he died are all still gone and none is
  given back. He is answerable as of ch 401, which is a third thing and worse than being sendable because an answer
  can be quoted, and the volume ended with him refusing a second use because the first one has not finished.**
- **No figure of what is left of him is stated on any page in ch 405–420, and the Ledger was not ordered, held,
  priced, refused or thought about.**
- **The surface answer was paid once at ch 267 and is not said, hinted at, gestured at or paraphrased in any mouth in
  Volume 7. The tenth part of the structural answer is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong.**
- **The relicensing legs stay in order — clearance, seal, countersignature — and Volume 7 invented none of them.
  MEMBER ABSENT stands three times, is permanent, and was not raised anywhere in Volume 7.**

| **The Hand, spent once and refused once** | **CLOSED FOR VOLUME 7 AND STILL RUNNING.** Spent at ch 402 on a listed clearing, out of her foundation, told before spent. Refused at ch 419 out loud, in thirteen sentences, because a second use is a second time to be sent for and the first one has not finished. **He closed the habit on the page: one is what he has and two would be a habit** | ch 401, 402, 419 |
| **She has given up knowing what the fine hand says about her** | **STILL OPEN FOREVER, ON PURPOSE, AND IT IS HERS.** A denial is an entry, so her no went up the same road every answer has gone up since before the mill's wheel was cast. **Nothing was written in that room, no book will not shut and nothing failed.** The bill at ch 418 is that the floor now treats her as somebody who may know something, four times a month, which is worse for her than being entered and was worth it | ch 416, 417, 418, 420 |
| **The six costs** | **ALL SIX LIVE AND NONE RE-DERIVED.** The furnace will not light itself · the hand cannot write a false line · a thing she reaches cannot be put back · she cannot be given anything · **she cannot be paid** · the reach will not go out of a room she has not been asked into. Each named once, in its own stage chapter | ch 371, 383, 394, 404, 412, 420 |
| **She cannot be paid, and the bare board was only ever a season** | **CLOSED FOR GOOD.** A column carrying a person past nine has no last day on it and no figure against it, so no office on nine days of road can enter her hours as wages. The board at the works rail is full at twenty-nine lines and every one of them is measured by the weight or by the length, and she can take neither | ch 412, 420 |
| **The reach answers a question and only a question** | **LIVE.** It went once in two hours on a lifting floor and it went when a man of thirty asked her for it, and **the last line of the volume is that the only thing she could do about any of it was be asked** | ch 420 |
| **A stair book going up a road once a month with a name in it in two books that shut** | **STILL OPEN.** She asked Rao to take her name out of it at ch 403 and was told no, in four sentences, by a woman who is right. **Both books shut and no hand in either building can take the line out** | ch 388, 389, 395, 396, 398, 403, 404 |
| **A page at a yard with a margin note in chalk in her own account, going up the mill road on the first and down on the ninth** | **STILL OPEN, and it is hers.** She wrote a line in pencil in the ordinary hand, dated it, and put it in beside a column. Rao did not say thank you and did not say anything else about it for about eleven minutes | ch 410 |
| **Geng's floor is a block short and a bell has already gone** | **OPEN AND CONSEQUENTIAL.** A dressed stone stands four feet from where it stood and cannot be put back, because she cannot be given a fix and does not want one | ch 419, 420 |
| **The man at the north end who said one thing about her in the middle of the second course and told nobody** | **STILL UNKNOWN TO HER.** She does not know he exists as anything but a man who took the weight on the bar. He put the finding in a margin and did not name her | ch 399, 403, 413 |
| **Rao's four hours a month, for nothing** | **OPEN AND ONGOING.** *You are here to read*, on the fourth, the eleventh, the eighteenth and the twenty-fourth at the fourth hour, and **being able to read is not a thing this ground has ever paid anybody for and is going to keep not being a thing this ground pays anybody for** | ch 410, 413 |
| **Wei Ran and the eight coppers** | **OPEN.** She is owed eight and cannot pay them, and her hand will not take them. Three carts went out of that bank on the twenty-eighth and did not go in | ch 407 |
| **The keeper with the paste pot and the book on the stone under his ladder** | **STILL OPEN AND UNTOUCHED.** He cannot read a word in it and will not shut it and would not tell anybody. **She did not go to the post in Volume 7** — ch 375 says so in her own decision, and ch 378 is the one time she walked past it with a roll of strips in her arms | ch 374, 375, 378, 379 |

### What Volume 8 inherits, live

**Every row above that is marked STILL OPEN, STILL TRUE, STILL CARRIED, NOT ON THE PAGE or STILL WORN is live and
inherits. In one list, for the Volume 8 planning phase to read first:**

1. **The Hunter's request, pending and unraised, and the line's own people still not knowing she exists**
2. **Everything above the offices that vouched — unread, unnamed, and no office named anywhere**
3. **The fine hand and the man who can read it — both spent at ch 417 and neither given back**
4. ***Kiln-born* in two heads and on no paper**
5. **What became of the eleven**
6. **The register still *reconciled*, and unread**
7. **The Clay Plate still worn and undescribed**
8. **The year of non-forgiveness still carrying, and unmentioned**
9. **The relicensed licence never opened · MEMBER ABSENT carried and never raised · Feng off the page · the ch 150
   refusal unsoftened · no second Lung and no second appointment · the relicensing legs unused and in order**
10. **The claim answered and not reopened · Hou Sanliang paid and never met · Ning Bo discharged · Ning Wu's decade a
    line with no mark on it — none of them on Volume 7's page**
11. **Yuan: readable without her, findable, held, sendable, and now answerable — his unentered state, his unnamed
    state and the date of the morning he died all still gone, and no figure of what is left of him stated anywhere**
12. **The Hand spent once and refused once, with the first use still running**
13. **And the volume's own: the strip that went east and did not come back · the column that came back with two lines
    off it · a stair book going up a road once a month with a name in it in two books that shut · a page at a yard
    with her own margin note going up and coming down · a floor a block short**

**What Volume 7 does NOT hand over, and may not be assumed:** the answerable question has been asked and not answered
and **there is no way on nine days of road to un-ask it**; she **cannot be paid**; **she cannot be given anything**;
**the reach will not go out of a room she has not been asked into**; and **she has never been east of here.**

### Sixteen findings the Volume 7 close recorded and did not repair

**These are not threads in the story. They are faults on the page, and a repair phase owns them.** Full table in
`state/current.md` under `## Volume 7 close audit — CLOSED` and in `state/continuity.md` under `## Volume 7 close —
repairs`: **four canon breaches** (six named weekdays at ch 391, 395, 399 and 400 · `Volume 6` in body prose at
ch 417 · `## Mouth Five` at ch 409 and `## Mouth Six` at ch 415 · a stray `Nai.` at ch 420) **and three internal
contradictions** (the Hunter's position at ch 369, 404, 418 and 420 · one walk at two lengths in ch 378 · an
unresolvable referent in ch 401's cost paragraph). **None of them touches ch 417, ch 420's last two sections, or the
volume's own answer, and if a repair appears to weaken any of those, the repair is wrong and the page is right.**

### The repair phase that owns them, and what it blocks

nothing in the phase system obliged anyone to take them, so `state/current.md` and `state/book-metadata.md` were

UNTIL IT HAS.** Eleven chapter files are to be reopened: **ch 369, 378, 391, 395, 399, 400, 401, 409, 415, 417 and

## Volume 8 PLANNED — what the plan adds to the thread list, before a line of it is on the page

**Recorded by the Volume 8 planning phase. `outline/volume-08.md` and `outline/batches/volume-08-batch-0001.md`
exist and are the authority for Volume 8. Nothing below is on the page yet. These are obligations a drafter inherits,
not facts a reader has been given.**

**REVIEWED AND REPAIRED before drafting; no thread in this list was added, removed or re-worded by the review.** Six
defects in the plan itself were closed, all of them figures or a title rather than a thread: the stage span (now **215
days**, day 1,076 to day 1,291, at intervals of 76 and 139, with 139 the gap and not the length of the climb), the
closing silence (**eighty-four days, day 1,256 to day 1,340, which is the figure already stated above and was the only
one that was ever right**), the ch 428 title, two stale day-table rows, one line of dialogue moved from ch 425 to ch
423, and a day that two chapters both claimed. **The three figures this section carries — ch 423 for the ask, ch 445 for
the origin, ch 462 for her making him stop — are all unchanged.** Record at `state/current.md` §*Volume 8 plan —
REVIEW REPAIR*.

### How Volume 8 pays what Volume 7 left

- **The ch 417 refusal is the volume's origin, and it is paid at ch 445.** She would not hear what the fine hand says
  about her, and **a person who will not hear a thing they were offered in a room is a person who can read it**, and
  that is how a room full of strangers read it correctly, and it is the reason the Order came to her door. **Her best
  decision in two volumes is the reason the worst thing that has happened to her is happening, and she is the only
  person who can say she did not expect it. Nothing is undone, nothing is softened, and ch 417 stands.**
- **The ch 401 answer is asked at ch 423**, by a stranger with the two lines and the date and the ordinary voice and no
  name at all. **The last line of Volume 7's protagonist answer — that this would happen — is now a chapter.**
- **Geng's second column, built at ch 420, is the instrument that catches Yuan at ch 455.** Two dates on one line,
  four days apart, on a floor sheet east of the turn. **It is her own and it is the volume's engine.**
- **The ch 418 bill — four people on that floor asking her a question a month — is closed at ch 478**, when they stop.

### What the plan adds

- **The Order of the Recruited**, named once at ch 425, and only ever one building, one book, one rate, one rule.
- **Xie Changyin**, sixty-one, recruited at nineteen, wrong about one thing and right about nine, and the sixth mouth
  at ch 477. **She returns in Volume 12 with a budget attached and that budget is not on the page here.**
- **Merit intercepted off people who are not her**, four times across the volume, and one man east of the turn who does
  not come back from a listing at ch 460. **It is Yuan's act and she finds out four days late.**
- **She makes him stop at ch 462**, out loud, on the boards, and takes nothing from him for eighty-four days.
- **The bill at ch 466**, in a number, unasked, once, and it is worse than she thought. **She does not forgive him and
  she does not tell him to stop, and the year is not mentioned once in fifty-eight chapters.**
- **Nascent Soul 9 at ch 470**, and the cost: **there is nothing above her and the way out is a document she has not
  opened.** The relicensed licence stops being a piece of paper she is carrying and becomes the only door in the world,
  **and Volume 8 does not open it.**
- **The refusal at ch 474**, on the record, with her name in it.
- **The silence at ch 478**: nothing on nine days of road wants anything from her at all, and she waits anyway.

### What the plan explicitly adds to *do not do*

- **Do not let the Order become a hall, a sect, a conspiracy, a court or a throne.** One building, one book, one rate,
  one rule, one lamp on every table.
- **Do not show a budget.** That is Volume 12's.
- **Do not let the Order explain why a bounded world needs readers.** The tenth part is not given and nothing proves
  the crews wrong.
- **Do not name an office**, and do not name a department, a floor, a rank or a wing of anything.
- **Do not put a bracket anywhere but ch 434 in Batch 0001 and ch 470 in the volume.**
- **Do not mention the year of non-forgiveness once.** The bill at ch 466 is a bill and not a forgiveness.

### The sixteen findings of the Volume 7 close

**Unchanged and still owned by a repair phase. F1–F7, the seven that are defects on the page, are now carried as
`## PHASE ZERO` at the head of `workspace/volume-08-batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, with the file and the line of each and the
one thing not to do while fixing it.** F8–F13 are state-file figures already corrected at
`state/continuity.md` §*Volume 7 close — the volume as a whole* and are to be verified. F14 is a false claim in
`outline/volume-07.md`, which is not to be re-planned by anything. **No thread in this file is affected by any of the
sixteen and none is retired by the Volume 8 plan.** The audit of why the planning phase opened ahead of the order is
at `state/current.md` §*Volume 8 planning — the deviation, and where the sixteen findings went*.

## Volume 8 Batch 0001 (ch 421–434) — what this batch added, and what is live going into Batch 0002

**Volume 8 is open on the page. Fourteen chapters, day 1,091 to day 1,152, 27,657 words of body prose, average ~1,976,
one bracketed layer line at ch 434, Nascent Soul 8 taken on the pinned day, and the batch's irreversible step at
ch 432. Nothing below is resolved.**

### New in this batch, and open

- **Xie Changyin is on the page and has asked nothing and bought nothing.** Sixty-one, eleven miles on foot, no folder,
  no men, no seal, no cart, no office behind her. She arrived on the second of a month at the seventh hour in daylight
  at a shed at a mill, put a folded sheet on the boards and did not open it, and said once: *I did not come eleven
  miles to ask a question.* **She is to be at a gate at the sixth hour on the twenty-ninth and she did not need an
  answer and did not want one.**
- **THE CH 401 ANSWER HAS NOW BEEN ASKED, AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME.** A woman with the two lines — *a line has not been
  carried to a room it was made out for* / *a holder is required against it* — and the date, the nineteenth of a month
  forty-three days back, and the ordinary voice. **She has no third thing and no name and she does not know there is a
  man. She is asking about a hand. He was asked and he cannot answer and nothing was put in a book.** He said in
  advance, at ch 401, that this would happen, and it happened.
- **She answered nothing and then said no out loud.** Working out in a doorway that the only way to answer that
  question correctly is to tell the truth, and that the truth is a thing she has never said out loud to anybody, and
  that saying it puts a voice in a stranger's book. **Nothing was written. Nothing failed. No book will not shut.**
- **The Order of the Recruited is named, once, at ch 425, in Xie Changyin's mouth.** One building, one storey, a hundred
  and forty feet front, a counter, a lamp on every table, about a hundred people reading, **no hall in it** · two
  coppers a page and two other rates · **a licence that is not a piece of paper that says what you are** · a room,
  nine feet by eleven, four of them and three empty for eleven years · standing · **a book with four hundred and six
  names on it, and a name on it is a person who cannot be un-recruited.** **No budget, and where the money comes from is
  not stated once and is not going to be in Volume 8.**
- **A strip came back down the mill road on a cart that does not go east**, on the twenty-eighth of a month, **with one
  date on it and nothing else**, dated the eighteenth of a month forty-four days back, in the ordinary hand that comes about
  nine times a year. **It is still on the seat board of that cart at the mill under a stone, and she did not take it and
  she did not sleep on it**, and it is the oldest thing she is carrying apart from the strip against her ribs.
- **A woman of sixty-one walks in from the far side of the turn and does not come back on a cart**, and **nobody who
  walks in from there has ever walked out again**, and a carter at a cart stand has thought about that once and has not
  thought about it since.
- **A date and a place are on the bottom of a folded sheet in a woman's coat.** The twenty-ninth, the sixth hour, the
  gate at the east end of the long road. **Entered in the ordinary hand and not read out.** Her name is not in it.
  **Nothing can un-agree it.**
- **SHE CANNOT CALL A HALT.** Named once, at ch 434, in her own thought. **Not the arm, not the hand, not the four
  fingers — herself, once a thing she is in has started.** She can still say no and she cannot make the no take, and
  she has been shown what that costs in a made road at the ninth hour with four ways to undo a date that all worked.
- **She spent her one question on a stranger and got a stranger's good name into a room of nine men.** Nine days of
  Geng's figures read by a young man with a good eye and no standing, **nothing wrong in any of them, said out loud**,
  the first time in nineteen years, and **Geng wrote his name at the foot of the sheet and did not thank anybody.** He
  also told her, after she had got to the door, that the floor was a block short before she came and is two blocks
  short now and that nobody has worked out how to write that down.
- **The Hunter has told her what a thing read by somebody else sounds like read back by the person it is about.**
  *Yes. It is not usually a good idea. He has watched it happen twice.* **He did not say who and she did not ask twice.
  That is the whole of that page and it is the first of his two pages in Volume 8.**
- **A man at the north end of a yard has asked four men a question about her out loud and has told nobody what the
  answer was**, and she does not know he exists as anything but a man who took the weight on the bar.
- **A woman of about fifty at a yard looked down nine hundred feet at a woman with an empty cup on a bench and decided
  in four seconds not to walk it.** That is the second time that shape has happened and it is the same shape both
  times, and it is on her boards.
- **She has a question she is allowed to ask, one thing about one person, not about herself**, and it will be answered
  in a room with a lamp in it, **and it may not be a question that would put a third person in a room who is not in
  it.** She has not asked it. **She has not chosen the thing yet and Batch 0002 must not have it chosen for her.**

### The batch's own reversals, paid on the page

- **A man with a rule in his hand said a number twice and she spent it out of the ground under her own feet** — six days
  and two hours, off two hundred yards of hedge east of the turn, third listing of the same ground, **not on a death**
  — and the ring did not come up at all for two days afterwards.
- **She spent the only question she had on Geng's floor sheet and got nothing for herself out of it**, and she did not
  know what to do with her face and she left the room.
- **She was turned back at a gate at the end of the long road, once, for no reason that belonged to her**, and she
  came through on the second day and the man said nothing about the first day to anybody.
- **She gave up eleven days at the lifting floor — the only thing on nine days of road that has ever made use of her —
  to go and stand at the end of a made road**, and Geng does not know why she stopped coming and does not ask.
- **She said a date out loud in daylight to a stranger and cannot un-agree it.**
- **She tried four ways to take the date back and all four of them worked and she used none of them**, and she said
  *I am not going* in a made road in daylight, and it was true, and it went nowhere.

### Held, confirmed, and not touched by this batch

- **The fine hand, and what it says about her. SPENT AT ch 417 AND NOT GIVEN BACK.** Xie Changyin says *I am not asking
  you about the strip* once, at ch 425, and does not ask, and does not know Ning Yan refused it. **Her ch 417 refusal is
  the reason the Order came and that is ch 445 in Batch 0002 and is not hinted at here beyond that one line.**
- **The Hunter's request is still pending.** A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. **The line's own people still do
  not know she exists** and Volume 8 is not the volume in which that stops being true.
- **Everything above the offices that vouched is unread and unnamed, and no office is named anywhere in ch 421–434.**
- ***Kiln-born* is in two heads and on no paper.** What became of the eleven is not raised. The four months erased are
  never named.
- **The register still reads *reconciled* and was not read once.** The Clay Plate is worn and **was not described** —
  the Clay Plate line was cut from ch 430 while drafting, because the volume's rule is that it is not described.
- **The year of non-forgiveness is still carrying and the subject was not mentioned once in fourteen chapters.** She
  does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.
- **The relicensed licence still says Foundation the first stage on the front of it and she has still never opened
  it.** The relicensing legs stay in order and none was invented and no card said one was or was not required.
  MEMBER ABSENT carried and never raised.
- **The claim is answered and not reopened. Hou Sanliang is paid and has never been met.** Ning Bo's account is
  discharged and **Ning Wu's decade is a line with no mark on it**, and **none of the three is on the page.**
- **The surface answer was paid once at ch 267 and is not said, hinted at, gestured at or paraphrased in any mouth in
  this batch.** **The tenth part of the structural answer is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong.**
- **Yuan's unentered state, his unnamed state and the date of the morning he died are all still gone and none is given
  back.** **No figure of what is left of him is stated on any page in ch 421–434.** The Hand was not spent and not
  refused. The Ledger returns **0**.
- **Wei Ran appears once in this batch and once in Volume 8.** It is a trade, a team, a rate and an opinion, and the
  eight coppers, and there is no harem and nothing a romance anywhere in these fourteen chapters.

### What Batch 0002 inherits, in one paragraph

**Day 1,153, the thirtieth. She is at Nascent Soul 8 with eleven coppers and no wages, a hundred yards short of a
turnstile and a question of her own she is allowed to ask, a strip still under a stone at a mill four miles west, a
carter at a cart stand who will say what the far side of the turn is, a turnstile man who has counted four hundred and
about forty, a woman of sixty-one who will be at that gate at the sixth hour, and a long low lime-washed building with
a lamp in every window and about a hundred people in it reading, and not one of them has looked up.** The Hunter has
one page left in the volume and she asks him on it. **The reach answers a question and only a question, and she can
still say no and cannot make the no take, and every one of those three facts is load-bearing in the next fifteen
chapters.**

## Volume 8 Batch 0002 (ch 435–449) — what this batch added, live going into Batch 0003

**Fifteen chapters, day 1,153 to day 1,205, 21,673 words, average ~1,445, zero brackets. Pressure: being wanted.** *Figures re-measured after the review repair of 14 Oct 2026; see `state/continuity.md` §*Repair pass — Volume 8 Batch 0002, after the review of 14 Oct 2026*.* Full record in `state/continuity.md` under *Established facts, Volume 8 Batch 0002 (ch 435–449) — BEING WANTED*.

- **The irreversible step is on the page at ch 445: she reads a fine hand in a room and it goes on a roll.** Another person's sheet, four failures before her, woman of twenty-two among them; name entered, four hundred and six to four hundred and seven; nothing can take it out. Ch 417 stands and is not softened.
- **Two new live threads from the repaired ch 445.** **The young reader of twenty-six argued against asking her to read the tied sheet and was right — in front of a room she becomes a hand in that room, and somebody will write it down in a year and call it her choice.** He is owed something by the room that overruled him and has not been told so. **The woman of twenty-two asked whether the hand was a bad one and was refused, because telling her would have been a confirmation, and a confirmation is an entry.** She went on not knowing, and she has a bad night in nine months' time. Ning Yan is the only person alive who knows both halves of that and cannot spend either.
- **The structural answer continues in two of six mouths: the reader of twenty-six at ch 437 and the turnstile man at ch 443, at the same turnstile, once, and not again.** The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong; the Order never explains why a bounded world needs readers; the ch 267 surface answer is never re-voiced.
- **The Hunter's two pages in Volume 8 are spent (ch 428, ch 440).** His request stays pending; the line's own people still do not know she exists. He is east of the turn and past the turnstile; no paper shown on the page in this batch.
- **Wei Ran's one card in this batch is spent at ch 441** as trade, team, rate, opinion and eight coppers held as credit. No harem, nothing a romance. One card in Batch 0004 remains.
- **Money closes at nine coppers, no bread.** The four-copper price is put down and swept back untaken; she cannot be paid and never could. The building's rate, room, standing, licence and roll are all stated; its budget is never stated.
- **Two yeses said without meaning (ch 448) and one silence held on purpose (ch 449)** carry into Batch 0003 with the four hundred and seventh name, the tied folder, the turnstile man's kept decision, the reader's unconfirmed rightness, and the woman of twenty-two with nine months to a bad night.
- **Held for Batch 0003 by record, not dropped:** the Hand spending on a man at a gate carded by the outline for this batch was forbidden by this batch's own prompt and is carried forward; Batch 0003 owns the listing surfacing at ch 453, the second column at ch 455, the fourth frame scene at ch 458, and the stop at ch 462.

### What Batch 0003 inherits, in one paragraph

**Day 1,205, the twenty-second. She is at Nascent Soul 8 with nine coppers and no bread, a reading on a roll she cannot take out, two yeses she did not mean, and a silence held in daylight to a woman of sixty-one who keeps a written count of the one time in ten she is wrong.** The building works on with a hundred readers, four hundred and seven names, folders dated on their strings, and a rate she cannot touch. The gate keeps its chain, cup, stool and kept decision. The road runs eleven miles west to a shed, a mill, a store and a strip still under a stone. **Batch 0003 opens day 1,206, the twenty-third, and owns what he did with her while she was somewhere else.**

## Volume 8 Batch 0003 (ch 450–464) — what this batch added, live going into Batch 0004

**Fifteen chapters, day 1,206 to day 1,262, 27,879 words, average ~1,859, zero brackets. Pressure: what he did with her while she was somewhere else.** *Re-measured after the Batch 0003 review pass of 2026-10-14; the drafting phase had 27,826 / ~1,855, and the money figures this block inherits were corrected in the same pass — the last copper went on the twenty-sixth of the month before last and the last loaf on the twenty-eighth, not on the eleventh.* *Figures measured by grep after the final edit; see `state/continuity.md` §*Established facts, Volume 8 Batch 0003 (ch 450–464)* and §*Construction budget — Volume 8 Batch 0003*. Full record there.*

- **THE IRREVERSIBLE STEP IS ON THE PAGE AT CH 462: SHE MAKES HIM STOP.** Out loud, once, in a shed with the door shut. He gives no promise, tells her why he told her once (*because you would have asked me not to do it*), and then **takes the fourth one and puts it down.** It cannot be undone. It cost her a man, four hundred yards of cart track she did not walk and four steps she did not go up, and it bought her nothing in coin and eighty-four days of nobody paying for anything. **She does not forgive him. She does not tell him she does. She does not thank him. The rest of the volume is the sound of a man with nothing to spend.**
- **THE HIMSELF-HABIT IS SPENT AND STATED, ONCE, AT CH 462.** Three spends in the batch, on the sixth, the fifteenth and the twenty-first of a month beginning 1,214, all out of the ground under her feet and identical in mechanism to ch 402. **He told her the number before the first one and told her nothing about the second or the third, and he has said on the page why: she would have asked him not to do it.** That is the whole of the omission and it is the thing the bill at ch 466 prices. **Batch 0004 owns the bill and must not re-argue the omission.**
- **THE LEFT COLUMN IS THE LAYER'S, AND NEITHER OF THEM KNOWS IT.** A listing carries a piece of work; work on this ground carries the day it happened and the day anybody found out; the layer does not distinguish and writes both, into a ruled cell on a floor sheet, four days before the floor knows anything. **She has worked out the four days and the twelfth line and has not worked out a cause, and the reader has not been told the cause in one place either.** Batch 0004 may not turn this into an explanation in Ning Yan's mouth.
- **THE DEATH IS ON THE PAGE AND IT IS HER FAULT AND IT IS NOT SOFTENED.** A man of fifty, nineteen years at a gate, found on the fourth day of a month at the fourth hour with his boots off and his coat on and nothing on him. **She said it out loud in a doorway, in daylight, to two women and a man in a grey shawl, and nobody in the room can check one word of it, and nobody believed her and nobody disbelieved her.** `kill` and `murder` return 0 across the batch and the prose says *he was not alive*. **A man of thirty-four was paid twice for a thing that happened once and has never looked at the book. He has a wife, a girl of nine, a rate and eleven years on a frame. He does not know and must not be told in Batch 0004.**
- **THE STRUCTURAL ANSWER CONTINUES IN TWO OF SIX MOUTHS:** the woman of fifty who runs a floor east of the turn (ch 452) and the man of thirty-four paid twice (ch 461). The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong; the Order never explains why a bounded world needs readers; **the ch 267 surface answer is never re-voiced and `drift` returns 0.**
- **THE ORDER'S FLOOR IS A REAL PLACE NOW AND IT IS NOT THE ORDER.** A lane two miles past the building, a cut, a frame, nine men, six carts a day, a woman of fifty, six day books, a shed, and a crossing a mile further with a painted board and a chain. **No office is named in any of it.** A gate man is there too and is the fourth of them in this volume. The Order's building, its rate, its roll and its budget rule are untouched by this batch and its budget is still never stated.
- **She is not hungry, and it is not a gift.** Stated once, at ch 464, and it is the first time this batch explains rather than reports. **She is not going to starve and nobody on nine days of road can be told why, and she closes the batch with five coppers, no bread since the twenty-eighth of the month before last, and a bar strap she rebound out of her own coat lining.**
- **Eighty-four days are now on the page, counted into a board at the end of a lane on day 1,261.** Day 1,262 minus the thirteenth of the month beginning 1,244 is the figure and **day 1,340 minus day 1,256 is eighty-four, which is the length of his silence.** The stages stall because a stage needs a second pair of hands in a room for hours and nobody is paying for one. **Batch 0004 opens day 1,263, the twentieth, at the same lane, and the silence is already running.**
- **The young reader of twenty-six has still not been told he was right.** He argued against asking her to read the tied sheet, the room overruled him, and nobody has said a word about it. **Not a chapter in this batch paid it and Batch 0004 does not have to either; it is carried, and if it is spent it must be spent as a small thing in somebody else's mouth and not as a scene.**
- **The woman of twenty-two still does not know what a hand said about her**, and her bad night is still nine months out. **Xie Changyin does not appear in these fifteen chapters and has not been told anything: not the stop, not the death, not the two figures.** She is carried as not having come down the road for four days.
- **Held for Batch 0004 by record, not dropped:** the Hand **refused once out loud, in a room**; the bill in a number, unasked; **Nascent Soul 9 at ch 470 on day 1,291, the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,274, at 139 days, the longest single interval in the manuscript**; the offer in full with a form on the table and a sheet to be read to her; **the refusal at ch 474 on the record with her name in it, read back to her before it goes in**; and at ch 478 nothing on nine days of road wants anything from her at all.

### What Batch 0004 inherits, in one paragraph

**Day 1,262, the nineteenth. She is at the end of a lane two miles past the Order's building, in a shed with a door that does not shut properly, at Nascent Soul 8, with five coppers, no bread since the twenty-eighth of the month before last, a bar strap bound out of her own coat, and nothing left to spend.** The floor runs the same. The woman of fifty has told her she knew who she was and has been not saying anything about it for ten days. A chair at the crossing is empty and a board will have six lines on it before the month turns. A man of thirty-four is carrying a frame with two figures against one line of his work that he has never looked at. Thirteen lines in a left column nobody on that ground fills, two of them hers in the sense that one of them has her day on it. He has said *I know* once, and *that is further than anybody has got* once, and nothing at all for six days. **Batch 0004 opens day 1,263 and owns refusing to be a tool.**

### Added by Volume 8 — the volume's own threads, all live at ch 478

- **Nascent Soul 7 → 9, and the ceiling of a realm.** Taken at ch 434 on day 1,152 and at ch 470 on day 1,291, at intervals of 76 and 139 days. **The ninth stage arrived without her asking and without her being able to stop it, and there is nothing above her and the door is a document and she has not opened it.** Two bracketed layer lines in fifty-eight; the budget is spent.
- **She cannot call a halt** — herself, once a thing she is in has started. Named once at ch 434 and **paid off in the worst place at ch 470**, where she says no out loud in a room and the no does not take. Not re-explained anywhere after.
- **He stopped putting work where a hand would find it, at ch 462, and she paid for it with eighty-four days of nobody paying for anything.** He has had nothing to spend since the thirteenth of the month beginning 1,244 and **has said nothing since ch 471**. Three spends, three people who were not her, one man dead four days before she worked out why.
- **The bill, at ch 466, in a number, once, unasked-for-a-second-time: one thousand one hundred and nine days and six hours**, of which twenty-one days and four hours is the work he put where a hand would find it. **She has not spent it, has not forgiven it and has not taken it back anywhere.** Her own finding, out of it: the standing draw is the price of being findable, and **she does not know who set the price and put it nowhere.**
- **The Order of the Recruited**, named once at ch 425 in a shed at a mill. One building, one counter, one book with four hundred and seven names, one rate, one lamp on every table, one rule. **No budget — Volume 12's. No hall, no department, no floor, no rank list, no committee, no house, no title beyond *recruited*.** Xie Changyin, sixty-one, on the page at ch 423, 425, 449, 468, 469, 470, 472, 473, 474 and 477. **She is wrong about exactly one thing and does not know it, and Ning Yan refused that sentence and nothing else.**
- **THE REFUSAL, ON THE RECORD, AT CH 474.** Her name in the best book on nine days of road with the third of a month against it, read back to her before it went in. **It cannot be un-entered.**
- **And the cost of the refusal, which is the volume's real ending: the Order stopped reading her.** The bench went empty on the seventh, the floor stopped asking about carts, the counter went back to asking about nothing, the folder will go east with nothing on it. **She bought her own anonymity with her own usefulness and it is the best thing that has happened to her in two years and she cannot say so out loud to anybody including him.**
- **Her ch 417 refusal is read correctly by strangers and is the reason the Order came.** Stated once, in her own thought, at ch 445, and paid off at ch 474 when she tells a room full of strangers that she is the only person on nine days of road who can read the hand they cannot.
- **Six structural-answer mouths**, at ch 424, 437, 443, 452, 461 and 477 — a carter, a reader of twenty-six, a turnstile man, a woman of fifty, a man of thirty-four, and Xie Changyin. **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong, and the Order never explains why a bounded world needs readers.**
- **The fourth frame scene is on the page at ch 458**, away from her hearing, in the layer's own reading-space. **It is the only marked present frame in the volume and every sentence Yuan speaks anywhere in Volume 8 is in the present tense.**
- **A man east of the turn does not come back from a listing, and it is his act and she finds out four days late.** Batch 0003's. Not softened and not referred to again in Batch 0004.
- **The line's own people stopped not knowing she exists, in Batch 0004, and it was not the Hunter's doing.** It was **a woman of twenty saying her name out loud in a road with four men unloading within nine feet**, plus eleven weeks of a woman of fifty checking her figures. Ch 468.
- **At the last page nothing on nine days of road wants anything from her at all.**

### Volume 9 inherits, live

**1. The relicensed licence, still unopened, and now the only door in the world.** Volume 8 raised what it is worth three times without reading it, and the word `licence` returns zero across all fifty-eight chapters. **She wants to open it and has not, and the reason is on the page.** **2. The refusal standing in the best book, unremovable.** **3. Nothing above her.** **4. He has nothing to spend and has stopped answering, and she is waiting anyway.** **5. A sheet on a cart east of the turn with a girl of twenty-two on it that nobody is going to read.** **6. The address of a shelf, a room and a date on a string, given and not asked for, scraped off a board and written again.** **7. Thirteen lines in a left column that the layer wrote and that no one on that ground can explain.** **8. A man of thirty-four with two figures against one line of his own work.** **9. A chair at a crossing under a cloth.** **10. The woman's of fifty's honesty — she wrote nothing down for nineteen years and she said so out loud.**

**The question Volume 9 opens onto is fixed at `outline/volume-08.md` §*New question for Volume 9* and is not re-derived here: she has spent a volume being the strongest person in every room and it turned out to be a qualification; she has refused the one place on nine days of road that would have paid her for the only thing she is good at; the price of the refusal is that there is now nothing on nine days of road that wants anything from her; and at the top of a realm, with the only door in the world being a piece of paper she has been carrying since a furnace took her father, she is at last free enough to go and open it. The question is not whether she will be recruited again. It is what a person does with a silence that she bought, when the one voice in her head has nothing left to spend and has stopped answering, and there is a door eleven hundred years of practice has never been opened from the inside.**

**And the last thing that happened on the last page of Volume 8, which is the first thing Volume 9 opens on: she said his name once into a shed with boards on the floor, and nothing came back, and she waited anyway.**

**2. The refusal standing in the best book on nine days of road, unremovable, with the third of a month against it.**
**3. Nothing above her.** Every technique she owns runs out at nine and there is no tenth and no hand on any road that
can take her one step further. **4. He has nothing to spend and has stopped answering, and she waited anyway.**
**5. A sheet on a cart east of the turn with a girl of twenty-two on it that nobody is going to read.** **6. The address
of a shelf, a room and a date on a string, given and not asked for, scraped off a board and written again.**
**7. Thirteen lines in a left column that the layer wrote and that no one on that ground can explain.** **8. A man of
thirty-four with two figures against one line of his own work, who has never once looked at them.** **9. A chair at a
crossing under a cloth with a light in the hut behind it.** **10. The woman of fifty's honesty — she wrote nothing down
for nineteen years and she said so out loud.** **11. The Hunter's request, still pending, now with a name on the page.**
**12. Xie Changyin's season, which continues whether or not Ning Yan comes.**

**The question Volume 9 opens onto is fixed at `outline/volume-08.md` §*New question for Volume 9* and is not re-derived

years of practice has never been opened from the inside.**

**And the last thing that happened on the last page of Volume 8, which is the first thing Volume 9 opens on: she said
his name once into a shed with boards on the floor, and nothing came back, and she waited anyway.**

## Volume 9 Batch 0001 (ch 479–493) — what this batch added, live going into Batch 0002

**Ch 479–493 are on the page and Volume 9 is open.** This file last stood at the Volume 8 close and had nothing for
Volume 9 in it; these are the threads the fifteen chapters added, all live at ch 493.

### Added by this batch

1. **The paper is open and unnamed.** Opened alone at ch 488, read at ch 489, still folded in four against her ribs.
   **It has never been called by a trade term and must not be.** What is on the inside describes a door eleven hundred
   years of practice never opened from the inside and leaves the opening to her. **She told nobody, including him.**
   Live, and it is the volume's engine.
2. **Severance 1, taken at ch 493 on day 1,412.** The ceiling is behind her by one stage. **The cost is named once —
   she cannot keep a place by staying in it — and is not to be re-derived in any later chapter.** A later chapter may
   carry it and may show what it costs.
3. **The shed stopped being hers.** She slept in it two years, barred the door with her own bar, worked the clay, slept
   nine hours in it, and began somewhere between the fifth and the sixth of a month to think of it as a place where she
   was. On the morning of the nineteenth she put her hand on the boards and said *this is mine* out loud and the reach
   did not answer it. **She left at the seventh hour of the twentieth and did not look back, and there is not one person
   on nine days of road who will ever know she was in it for two years.**
4. **The silence is eighty-five days and counting, and he has not been on the page once.** He does not appear in any of
   the fifteen chapters. The run is measured from day 1,256. **It is not broken until ch 520.**
5. **The Hunter's first of two appearances is spent, at ch 491.** He is nine feet off a track and she stops twelve.
   **He answered her and did not write it down, and she saw him not write it.** One appearance remains in Volume 9 and
   she asks him both times. His name may be used once and is now used — or not; the drafted chapter calls him *he*.
6. **The store woman is the first structural mouth, at ch 484**, and the volume has four more to come.
7. **She has three coppers, no wages, no bread and no season**, and a bar she cannot hold at the heavy end. Nine days
   of road west have stopped wanting anything from her, which ch 478 found and Batch 0001 confirmed on the page.

### What Batch 0002 inherits, in one paragraph

**ch 494–508, days 1,413–1,488, seventy-six days, pressure *being written down*.** A man of nine hundred years walks

## Volume 9 Batch 0002 (ch 494–508) — what this batch added, live going into Batch 0003

**Ch 494–508 are on the page. Pressure this batch was *being written down*, and it is now in the last two chapters of
Volume 9.** The threads below are the ones the fifteen chapters added, and they are all live at ch 508.

### Added by this batch

1. **The copy, at ch 502. The batch's irreversible step and the volume's engine.** Her ch 474 entry, in her own words,
   said out loud in daylight on a bench at a crossing, **written in a nine-hundred-year-old hand with the date and with
   the place she named — the shed at the end of the lane.** It is a *page about her*, not the entry; the entry proper is
   kept or lost and is Batch 0004's at ch 536. **It cannot be un-copied.** Live, and Batch 0003 may not soften it.
2. **Severance 2, taken at ch 508 on day 1,488**, the fourth hour, alone, on open ground. **The cost is named once — she
   cannot be met where she is expected — and is not to be re-derived.** The reach answered *what am I at* with a shed.
3. **The paper is not the door.** Established at ch 488 and named out loud at ch 503, in her words and then in his: *that
   is the only door in the world* / *no*. **Still folded in four against her ribs, still unnamed by any trade term, still
   read and still not opened.** The opening of the real door is ch 518's and Batch 0003's.
4. **The rule, in full, and what it will not say.** A book, a rule older than she is, two places a name can go, kept or
   lost, the bound goes around a list and does not know it is there, and **no reason given.** **He has refused the
   question why a bounded world needs readers twice — at ch 499 and at ch 503 — and must not be made to answer it again
   in Volume 9.** His argument is spent once and is not repeated.
5. **Shuyin is on the page and is correct, and he is wrong about exactly one thing he has not said.** He is not a
   villain, he keeps no staff, he is not in a hall, and **he has not said that a person on his list is better kept than a
   person off it — that sentence is ch 531's and is Batch 0004's.**
6. **The misreading.** Eleven people on nine days of road have decided something has come for her and that she is inside
   it. **Four days old at ch 508 and it has two months to run.** She will not correct it, because correcting it means the
   page. **This is the volume's next pressure and Batch 0003 opens on it.**
7. **She has told nobody where and has stopped sleeping in the place on the page.** Three nights in three places,
   deliberately. **She is now findable by a list and not by a road, and she did that to herself without deciding to.**
8. **The stone under the keeper's ladder, nine miles west, with a book of three names in it that no office asked for.**
   **She did not ask him for it** and has thought about that twice. **Ch 538 leaves her own book on that stone**, and the
   setup for it is now on the page.
9. **Merit settles once, at ch 495**, on a leg fitted for nothing. **No figure of him is given and no figure of what is
   left of him is stated on any page.** He has not been on the page in fifteen chapters and the run from day 1,256 stands
   at **232 days** and is not broken until ch 520.
10. **Wei Ran, nine coppers a head, three times out loud, carried twice, eight coppers never chased.** One of Volume 9's
    two cards. The other is Batch 0004's.
11. **The body.** Right arm open four days in five and it is a rate. Thinner. Not hungry no more and that is finished.
    **Eighteen years old, nineteen before this volume closes.**

## Volume 9 Batch 0003 (ch 509–523) — what this batch added, live going into Batch 0004

**Ch 509–523 are on the page and Volume 9 is on its last batch.** The threads below are the ones the fifteen chapters
added, and they are all live at ch 523.

### Added by this batch

1. **Severance 3, taken at ch 523 on day 1,561**, the fourth hour, alone, on open flat ground at a turn, with the
   batch's one bracketed layer line. **The cost is named once — she cannot go back down the road she came — and is not
   to be re-derived.** 1,561 − 1,488 = 73; **Volume 9's bracket budget of three in sixty is now three spent, at ch 493,
   ch 508 and ch 523, and the volume carries nothing else in square brackets.**
2. **The wall, opened at ch 518 from the inside, on her order, and never closed.** **A wall opened from the inside is a
   document read from the inside by the person it concerns, and there has to be one pair of hands in the room, and he
   read it.** **He steps through and one person stays on the wrong side, permanently, and it is him.** **The opening is
   four feet wide and six feet high with a jamb on each side and it costs her nothing and it is not counted for her.**
   **He cannot come back across it.**
3. **The fifth frame scene, at ch 518**, inside the opening and away from her hearing, present tense throughout, about a
   door and not about being found, sent for, asked or spent. **The answer it finds — that a wall opened from the inside
   always leaves whoever opened it on the wrong side of it — is the same answer he put on a page at ch 516.**
4. **The price, in a number of one, put in front of her in writing at ch 516 and not said.** A clean page, one figure,
   **no heading, no date, no name and no second line.** **She left it on the bench in the dew because her hand will not
   take a gift.**
5. **His first words to her since ch 471, at ch 520: four words, at a gate, in daylight, in front of three people she
   did not ask for — *It was mine.*** **The run from day 1,256 is broken and was published three times first: 258 at ch
   516, 265 at ch 518, 273 at ch 520, each with its subtraction in the same sentence.** **It saves nobody and she is not
   grateful and does not thank him.**
6. **The misreading has ended, at ch 521, and it ended because the truth turned out to look like it.** Nothing on nine
   days of road wants anything from her and nothing ever will; the carrier says good at ten feet; the carter asks
   nothing; the store woman leaves a loaf on the counter and does not take it off again; the woman of fifty shuts her
   door. **The last bread the man who stopped coming did not pay for is still on the shelf and is still not given away.**
   **She is nineteen.**
7. **The chair.** It is in a nine-foot room against the west face of the wall and she has not sat in it since the
   thirteenth of the month beginning 1,514. **It is the first empty chair in this book that is not a death and it is
   worse than one, because the man is alive and cannot come back.**
8. **The room, the wall, the turnstile, the gate and the hut, measured and placed on the page** and recorded in full at
   `state/continuity.md` §*Established facts, Volume 9 Batch 0003*. **She has gone past the bar on two posts once and
   Batch 0004 does not bring her back across it.**
9. **The merit settles once more, at ch 512, and is intercepted**, and the finding is that a settling needs a name and a
   man and a figure and that she had none of the three. **No figure of him on any page.**
10. **The work.** A painted mark at the eighth foot of a frame four hundred years old, a stone four men could not get
    over, a board with forty-one lines and a space with a line on it, and a woman of forty-one who writes nine names a
    shift and asks nothing. **She can still do anything on this ground and nothing on it will hand her a line.**
11. **Structural mouth four of five, spent at ch 514** in a boy of fifteen with a chain and a cup, and **the fifth
    mouth is Batch 0004's, at ch 531, and it is Shuyin's.**
12. **The stone under the keeper's ladder, nine miles west.** Still there, still not asked for again, and **ch 538 is
    where a book of her own goes onto it.**

The copy at ch 502, which cannot be un-copied · Severance 2's cost · the rule in full and the question Shuyin will not

### What Batch 0004 inherits, in one paragraph

**ch 524–538, days 1,562–1,598, thirty-seven days, pressure *surviving being kept*.** She is at Severance 3 with no
place, no road and one sheet of paper against her ribs, and a man who keeps a book is on the wrong side of a wall he
opened for her and cannot come back across it. **The demand at its maximum is therefore made at the opened wall, in
daylight, with his book on a plank table carried out to the near side and him standing on his own side of the opening,
and she does not cross back** — recorded at `state/continuity.md` §*Three readings taken against the outline*, item 3,
so that Batch 0004 does not draft a contradiction and then repair it. **Ch 531 is Shuyin's mouth and the fifth and last
of them, five sentences he shuts the book on and then a sixth, and the accuracy is the whole of it.** **Ch 534–535 is the survival, written in her own hand where his cannot
take it out, and she refuses kept.** **Ch 536 he writes her as lost, shuts the book, and walks her to the door of the
room, and he stays where he is.** **Ch 537–538 she carries her own book west to the stone under the keeper's ladder nine
miles west and leaves it there, and the reason for the wall is recorded where he cannot reach it.** **The Hunter is in
this batch, once, at ch 531, and she asks him whether a person written as lost is safer than a person not written at
all, and he says he does not know, and then says the other thing, which is that she asked him, and that this is the
second question anybody has ever asked him that he would rather not answer.** **Wei Ran has her second and last card
of the volume here: nine coppers a head, said out loud, a trade and a team and a rate and an opinion, and nothing else.**
**Ning Wu does not appear.** The cards are at `workspace/volume-09-batch-0004/PROMPT.md`.

## Volume 9 Batch 0004 (ch 524–538) — what this batch added, live at the volume close

**Ch 524–538 are on the page and Volume 9 is closed on the page.** The threads below are the ones the fifteen chapters
added, and they are all live at ch 538.

### Added by this batch

1. **The rule, written once, at ch 536: *lost*, with the date, in his hand, and it stays in.** A name goes in when a
   person gets to the top of a realm with nothing to take them past it, once, as kept or as lost. **She is entered.
   She is not kept.**
2. **Her own book, on the stone under the keeper's ladder nine miles west, at ch 538.** Eight leaves, a date and a
   place, four sentences in her own hand on paper she made, six empties. Unasked, unkept-for, beside his book with an
   inch of stone between. **The reason for the wall is recorded where he cannot reach it, for the first time in nine
   hundred years.**
3. **Shuyin's mouth spent, at ch 531, the fifth and last structural mouth.** Five sentences he shuts the book on, and a
   sixth after the strap is in his hand, the one he is wrong about, said once. He is alive, old, nine miles west on the last
   page, nine miles west of the only room in which what he keeps is
   kept, the wall still open behind him — **and he is not thanked.**
4. **The Hunter's second appearance spent, at ch 531**, and his name with it. His request stays pending to the last page.
5. **Wei Ran's second card spent, at ch 527.** Nine coppers a head, the bar in the cart and not in the rate, and the
   opinion. Eight coppers unchased and nine unchased with them.
6. **The thirty-seven days, filled.** Paper made, a demand heard in full at the wall, a refusal read back out loud, a
   road walked west one last time going nowhere she has been. **No place left that is hers, and the volume's pressure is
   paid off, not carried.**
7. **The merit does not settle anywhere in this batch. No Hand is spent. Door is not opened.** No figure of what is left
   of him on any page, before or after. **Yuan is not on the page and his name does not appear.**
8. **“Twenty-two days,” said at the jamb at ch 536, and never placed. OPEN, carried to Volume 10.** He says it on the
   way out after writing her in as *lost* — two words, then “The chimney draws,” and she answers neither. The narration
   marks it as **the only thing he said that day she has not been able to place anywhere in nine hundred years**, and she
   has not gone over it since. It is deliberately unexplained on the page: it counts neither days past (fifteen remain to
   the far ground), nor the volume's thirty-seven (already spent), nor any distance in the book. **Treat it as a planted
   question, not an error — do not resolve it in a repair pass, and do not let a later batch retcon it into a figure
   already on the page.**

where three people watched her put it down and say his name and he answered.** Every thread the volume opened is carried

costs named once each and never re-derived · the fifth entry and the four before it · the copy at ch 502 that made her

## VOLUME 9 CLOSE REPAIR — what the repair moved, and what it deliberately did not move

**The close audited sixty chapters and recorded twenty-six findings. The repair cleared them and this section says, in one
paragraph, which of the threads the volume carried are standing differently afterward and which are exactly where they
were. It opens no thread and closes none.**

**No thread the Volume 9 close listed moved.** The three Severance costs are named once each and were not re-derived ·
the fifth entry and the four before it stand · the copy at ch 502 that made her findable by a list stands, and the entry
it copied is **byte for byte the entry it was before the repair** · the paper against her ribs, opened alone at ch 488 and
never named, stands · the opening at ch 518 with him on the wrong side of it alive stands · the chair nobody has sat in
since the thirteenth stands · the board with no line on it for three months now says **three months** in the one place
that used to say eleven weeks, which is the same claim · the Hunter's request is still pending and his notebook is still
shut · the ten parts not given and the surface answer never re-voiced stand · the ten coppers and the eight unchased
stand. **And *“Twenty-two days,” said at a jamb at ch 536 and never asked, is still OPEN, still unplaced, still
unexplained, and this repair did not touch that line.** The plant travels to Volume 10 exactly as the volume handed it
over.

**Two things did move, and both are worth a reader of the thread file knowing.** **First, the account she has been
carrying since ch 100 is no longer named at ch 502.** The outline gives the volume an allowance of zero for the subject of
non-forgiveness and states the strongest form — *not mentioned once in sixty chapters* — and the page had one sentence
that broke it. **The sentence is gone and nothing was softened: the fact it carried, that the account is not over and was
never hers to finish, is still on the page in her own words and was said out loud once on a made road.** Nothing at ch 466
or ch 474 was opened. **Second, the light in the hut went out on a night she was not there for, and she does not know
that.** ch 536 now says so in its own words — she was at a crossing eleven miles off it and has not asked — and **that is
a thread the repair opened, because ch 537:121 already stands as the narrator saying *dark now for the second night* from
the far side of her, and the two halves now agree.** Volume 10 may use it or leave it; **it is not a question she has been
asked and it is not a thing she knows.**

**And one question the repair is carrying forward, because it belongs to the planner and not to a drafter:**
`outline/volume-09.md` allows Wei Ran *at most two cards in Volume 9, one in Batch 0002 and one in Batch 0004*, and she is
on the page in three chapters — ch 497, ch 505 and ch 527, twenty-two occurrences on word boundaries. **A trade, a team, a
rate, an opinion, eight coppers unchased, no gift, no scene, nothing a romance, and the rate said out loud the same way
three times; Batch 0002 simply carries one card more than the allowance.** No phase may edit an outline, so this is
recorded for `workspace/volume-10-planning/PROMPT.md` to carry or to forgive, and **Volume 10's own allowance should be
set with the real figure of Volume 9's in the sentence beside it.**

**The one outline deviation the repair did not touch and could not:** `outline/volume-09.md` pins *four or five short
sentences each* across five structural mouths and **ch 484's mouth delivers nine.** Four of five hold. **The fifth is a
structural mouth and the repair order forbids touching one, so it is recorded and carried.**

## VOLUME 10 PLANNED at ch 538 — what Volume 9 hands Volume 10, in one paragraph

**Volume 9, *The Warden*, ch 479–538, day 1,341 to day 1,598, sixty chapters, 113,811 words of body prose on
method B, is closed, audited and repaired on the page, and Volume 10, *The List*, ch 539–600, is planned in
`outline/volume-10.md` with its first batch card at `workspace/volume-10-batch-0001/PROMPT.md`.** She stands four
feet off a ladder nine miles west of a turn with her own eight leaves on a stone and his answer in her ears,
entered as lost and refused as kept, at Severance 3 with no wages and no road; he stands wrong-sided with a bag
and a brush, findable, sendable and answerable, never thanked; the wall stays open with a table standing in it
and a window dark now for nights she does not know about. **What travels live:** the three Severance costs named
once each · the fifth entry and the four before it · the paper opened alone and never named · the book under the
ladder with her hand in it · the Hunter's request pending · the ten parts not given and the surface answer never
re-voiced · the plate worn and undescribed · the year still carrying, unmentioned · and **“Twenty-two days,”
said at a jamb at ch 536 and never asked, still OPEN, still unplaced, still unexplained, and no card in Volume 10
may equate it to any dated interval.** **What the plan adds, and it is a paragraph and not a page:** Volume 10
spends Severance 4 → 6 with three new costs, asks the list shut across four batches, shuts Shuyin's book in
daylight with no page read and no release shown, finds Ning Wu in person once and late with nothing settled or

against two) and F2 (ch 484's nine-sentence mouth) are carried, not forgiven, into the new outline's own
allowances. **This section opens no thread the chapters do not carry and previews nothing `outline/ending.md`
owns.**

## VOLUME 10 BATCH 0001 (ch 539–554) — what the batch opened, moved and closed

**Sixteen chapters, days 1,599–1,628, pressure *asking what else is on it*. Every line below is checked against the
page at `chapters/volume-10/`, not against a plan.**

### CLOSED by this batch

1. **Whether she can ask.** OPEN for eight years, from the second volume. **CLOSED at ch 546 and proved at ch 553.**
   She rehearsed on a boy of eleven and a foreman, said four words to a trough six times, and then said them to a man
   in daylight with a person hearing it. **The mouth works. That thread is finished and it is not to be reopened.**
2. **What she wanted from the answer.** OPEN since ch 525 — she did not want an answer, she wanted to be asked for.
   **CLOSED at ch 546 and ch 552: what she wanted was to be the person who asks, and the count she kept was never
   going to tell her whether to do it.** *A count is not a veto.*
3. **Whether the flat stone is a safe place.** OPEN since ch 538. **CLOSED at ch 544, by a carter who had nine days to
   think about it and thought about none of it, and then by a boy of eleven who cannot stop looking at it.**
   **A thing put down in the open is put down where it will be found.**
4. **Whether being seen is being written.** NEW at ch 551 and closed in the same chapter. She wanted her name in a
   flat book with three names in it, wanted it badly, and worked out inside a day that a name in that book is a day and
   a year and nothing else. **Being seen is not being written, and she has had it for eleven days without knowing it
   was anything.**

### OPEN, and now carried

5. **The question she asked and the answer to it.** **NEW at ch 553–554 and the volume's live engine.** *What else is
   on it*, said at the seventh hour of the twenty-fourth, gone in the book, **not answered.** She gets one at a time,
   at the wall, in daylight, and she does not get a second until the first is answered. **No card in Batch 0002
   resolves the first question; the closing day at ch 569–570 is what comes back.**
5a. **NEW at ch 554 — whether asking a question that is not about the list is free.** He answers *why those three* at
   the seventh hour of the twenty-fifth, in the same breath as the terms, and it is not a thing she said about
   whether that counts. **She cannot find out by asking, because asking is a question and the first one is still
   open.** Standing, unresolved, and carried into Batch 0002 as a live uncertainty rather than a rule.
6. **The hut light, a hundred yards off the wall.** Carried from ch 536 unlearned. **Batch 0001 saw it in daylight
   for the first time (ch 548), watched it for four minutes, confirmed it did not change, and learned nothing.** She
   has four questions she could put about it and has decided not to spend the first asking on any of them (ch 549).
   **STILL OPEN, and it is deliberately still open.**
7. **Twenty-two days.** **STILL OPEN.** Ch 550 puts the word *lost* on it and it comes apart at once; ch 552 prices it
   as a blank she has written her own name against. **No card equates it to a dated interval and Volume 11's door
   depends on it staying unplaced.**
8. **What *lost* does.** OPEN since ch 536. **Narrowed at ch 550 by one subtraction and not by an answer:** it is not
   about Ning Wu, because Ning Wu is a line with no mark and was never on a page, and a word written about an entry
   cannot be about him. **That is the whole of what three nights bought.**
9. **The brother's name.** `Ning Wu` **1** in the batch, at ch 550, in her own thought. **Ch 552 puts a new thing on
   it: she has decided she will not say it out loud in front of another person at a wall — not at the first asking and
   not at the second if it can be helped. That is the first time in nineteen years she has chosen to leave something
   shut on purpose, and it is carried into ch 585.**
10. **The list itself.** Asked about, entered, closing. **Shayin's terms are standing and they do not move. The
    closing day is named at ch 569–570 and the reversal is that her asking is what set it.**
11. **Whether the work pays for anything.** OPEN since ch 543. Eleven sacks, forty up onto a sill, three days of stone,
    a loaf a day and a half. **Something came out of her foundation at the ninth hour of the eighth (ch 545) and it
    was not in proportion to the weight or the length, and she still does not know what it bought.**

### CARRIED and untouched

12. The three Volume 9 costs — carried and shown at ch 541, 542 and 552, and **not re-derived anywhere**.
13. The paper against her ribs, opened alone at ch 488 and never named — **not touched, not described.**
14. The plate worn and undescribed — `plate` **0** in the batch.
15. The fifth entry and the four before it · the copy at ch 502 · the ten parts not given · the surface answer never
    re-voiced · `Ledger` **0** · the year of non-forgiveness **0 mentions** · the Hunter's request pending with the
    Hunter **not on the page in this batch**, which is correct, since Volume 10 allows him twice and both are in
    Batches 0002 and 0004.
16. **Him: findable, sendable, answerable, wrong-sided, and no figure of what is left of him on any page.**
    `Yuan` **0**, and he does not speak anywhere in the sixteen chapters.

### Two things the batch did that the next one must not undo

- **She has never thanked him and she is not going to.** Ch 543 records her withholding a thank from the two women
  who were paid, and ch 546 records her saying thank you to a boy of eleven who answered a question about a rung.
  **Not one of them is a thank to a man of nine hundred years, and no card in Volume 10 may write one.**
- **The batch's irreversible step is hers and it is spoken.** From ch 554 the list is closing. **Batch 0002 opens on a
  woman who has already asked, has got terms, and has not got one word of an answer.**

## VOLUME 10 BATCH 0002 (ch 555–570) — what the batch opened, moved and closed

**Sixteen chapters, days 1,629–1,696, pressure *being counted*. Every line below is checked against the page at
`chapters/volume-10/`, not against a plan.**

### CLOSED by this batch

1. **Whether she can be counted.** OPEN from ch 556. **CLOSED at ch 556 and shown at ch 557.** She cannot be counted
   where she stands; a foreman with a finger goes over the place she is standing, twice, and says *you are standing in
   the gap*. **The cost is real and it is bread:** a gang fed by a number of hands cannot feed her, and the work she
   found to replace it does not notice her either.
2. **Whether the flat stone is a safe keeping.** OPEN since ch 538. **CLOSED at ch 563, and it closed badly.** She took
   the book off it in daylight with the keeper watching. **The stone is a stone and it cannot be put back the way it
   was**, because a keeping that has been asked about is not kept the same way twice.
3. **What one question at a time is.** OPEN since ch 554. **CLOSED at ch 565 and it is the batch's engine.** It is not a
   queue. **He does not have the rest of it while one question stands open, and the one standing open is hers.** Every
   asking narrows; asking twice is the only lever and it closes everything.
4. **Whether a closing is a punishment.** NEW at ch 557. **CLOSED at ch 561 by a woman of sixty at a bench**, in six
   sentences: a keeping shuts on a day and nobody shuts it on anybody. **Not a punishment, and not about her** — and
   worse than that, because it does not care who is standing in front of it.
5. **Whether she would ask the boy of fifteen a second time.** OPEN from ch 553. **CLOSED at ch 567 (she did not) and
   reopened deliberately at ch 570 (she did, once, because a date should be heard by somebody else).**

### OPEN, and now carried into Batch 0003

6. **THE CLOSING DAY. Day 1,802, the nineteenth of the month beginning 1,784. 106 days remain at day 1,696.** Named at
   ch 570, **caused by her asking**, and every asking from that day narrows what can still be found.
7. **Four questions standing in the book, none answered, and she is not allowed a fifth.** The first is holding the rest
   of it shut. **Batch 0003 must not resolve any of them**; the volume's paying beat is ch 585.
8. **The lever.** She has found that asking twice is the only thing that would make him get to the first question
   sooner, and that it is also the only thing that would make it never get answered. **She has decided not to pull it,
   on purpose.** A live wire, and it belongs to ch 585 rather than to an earlier chapter.
9. **The hundred and six days and no work that pays.** She has no wages, no place, a stone face fourteen miles off, and
   forty-four miles a question. **The brother's name is still unsaid at a wall and she knows that not saying it is an
   absence and not a holding** — and that is exactly the thing ch 572's gift and ch 585's naming are about.
10. **What *lost* does.** Still unanswered. Four nights and one subtraction across two batches.
11. **The hut light, a hundred yards off the wall.** **STILL OPEN and deliberately untouched by this batch.** Four
    questions she will not spend the asking on.
12. **Twenty-two days.** **STILL OPEN**, unplaced, and no card in this batch equated it to a dated interval.
13. **Whether the work pays for anything.** OPEN since ch 543. Narrowed at ch 568 and not answered: **it came out of
    her on the eighth and the twenty-sixth of the month beginning 1,604, both times on listed work cleared past the
    line, and not once on any work a store is paying a figure for.**

### CARRIED and untouched

14. The three Volume 9 costs and the first Volume 10 cost — **carried and shown, not re-derived.**
15. The paper against her ribs, opened alone and never named — **not touched, not described.** There are two things

16. The plate worn and undescribed — `plate` **0**. The register still *reconciled* — **0**. *kiln-born* **0**. The

17. The Hunter's request pending · the tenth part not given · the surface answer never re-voiced · `Ledger` **0** · the

18. **Him.** **Not on the page in this batch at all.** `Yuan` **0**. He opens no wall, no figure of what is left of him is
