    untouched and Batch 0003 owns it.**

### Two things the batch did that Batch 0003 must not undo

- **She is keeping something for the first time in her life and nobody asked her to**, and she has decided not to write
  in the six empty leaves. Both are hers and both must still be hers at ch 570 of Volume 10.
- **The bill at the midpoint is hers and it is enormous:** the only thing keeping the list open for nine hundred years
  was nobody looking at it, she asked, and she has a hundred and six days to find a brother with an instrument that
  narrows every time she uses it. **Nothing is undone and nothing is softened and ch 536 stands.**

review repair recorded at `state/continuity.md` §*Volume 10 Batch 0003 — REVIEW REPAIR*, which reopened the sixteen
files and changed four figures this section carried.**

### CLOSED by this batch

1. **Whether a name said at a wall can be taken back.** OPEN from ch 566 and from the boy of eleven's sentences at
   ch 577 — **eight of them, in five speeches, which is the count the Volume 10 close established from the page.** **CLOSED at ch 585.** She said **Ning Wu** out loud at the seventh hour of the twenty-fifth of the month
   beginning 1,724, in daylight, four feet into an opening, on her own side, with a boy of fifteen four feet off and a
   carter four hundred yards east. **He did not write it.** It cannot be un-said. **Eight years of one thing kept shut
   on purpose ended with nothing bought.**
2. **What a told place actually is.** NEW at ch 572. **CLOSED at ch 578.** A person who tells her where somebody is
   hands her the place they were. **The gift and the loss are one motion: every direction anybody has ever given her
   points west, and a girl who has four miles west to walk is a girl who is not walking east.** She walked past the shut
   door and kept walking to a store. **After the repair this is no longer an uncaused fact: it is now the visible working
   of Severance 5, whose cost — she cannot walk toward a name she is given — is in her own mouth at ch 572 and is
   thirty-six hours old at ch 573.**
3. **Whether a loaf given without being asked can be put anywhere.** NEW at ch 575. **CLOSED at ch 581.** It is not a
   keeping, not a debt, not wages and not a rate. **A loaf is given. A place is kept. That difference is the whole of
   it**, and she has never been looked after by a person in her life.
4. **Whether a count can still be a plan.** OPEN from ch 569–570. **CLOSED at ch 582.** Seven counts in two days, the
   first costing a quarter of an hour and the last a minute and a half, and the answer did not move once. **The count
   has stopped being a way of finding out whether a thing can be changed and is now a way of passing between one
   number and the next.** **After the repair this closes harder than it did: at ch 586 she writes five numbers out of
   thirty-one and leaves twenty-six days out of a column on purpose, for the first time in her life, and tells nobody.**
5. **What the frame scene goes looking for.** OPEN from `outline/volume-10.md`. **CLOSED at ch 584.** **A name cannot
   be held open past the closing except by somebody left permanently on the wrong side of it, and the only such person
   on nine days of road is a nineteen-year-old with a bar.** He has looked for a second keeper and a second instrument
   for four hours and there is neither.

### OPEN, and now carried into Batch 0004

6. **THE CLOSING DAY. Day 1,802, the nineteenth of the month beginning 1,784. 31 days remain at the last page of this
   batch.** Named at ch 570, **caused by her asking**, and every asking from that day narrows what can still be found.
   **Batch 0004 must not re-name it, re-derive it or extend it.**
7. **THE BROTHER. He is alive, and she has not found him, and she has closed off asking.** He has never been on this
   volume's page in person and **Batch 0004 is the only place in the whole novel where he may be.** **Nothing on the
   page places him.** Everything anybody has told her about him is in the past tense and four miles west of a turn: a
   cottage at a stopped lime bed, a mill one mile on, and **a carter with a board who took four people east nine years
   back and did not come back for a long time, with the boy walking behind the last barrow because he would not get up
   on one.** She has decided, at ch 585, to find him herself and not to spend one of her four questions on it.
8. **Four questions standing in a book nine hundred years deep, none answered, and she is not allowed a fifth.** The
   first was asked on the twenty-fourth of the month beginning 1,604, **day 1,627**, and has been standing a hundred
   and twenty-one days. **Batch 0004 must not resolve any of them except by the terms he gave at ch 554, and the paying
   beat is the closing.**
9. **The lever.** Asking twice is the only thing in the world that would move the first question and the only thing
   that would stop it forever. **She found it on the nineteenth of a month beginning 1,634, day 1,652, which is now its
   single date on the page, and she has carried it on purpose for a hundred and nineteen days and has not pulled it,
   including on the twenty-fifth.** It is live and it belongs to the reader as a standing wire; Batch 0004 records
   whether she pulls it and it may not be spent early and for nothing.
10. **What she has put INTO him.** Four sentences about a house with a standing stone, taken at ch 580 and not put
    down. **It is in him and not in the book, and it will be true on the closing day whether or not it is true
    tomorrow, and she does not know how that instrument works.**
11. **What *lost* does.** Still unanswered. Four nights and one subtraction across two batches.
12. **The hut light, a hundred yards off the wall.** **STILL OPEN and deliberately untouched by this batch.** Its four
    questions are unspent and this batch established their price — **they are not outside the terms, they cost a trip,
    and there are not three of those trips left in the days she has.** Batch 0004 must not resolve it.
13. **Twenty-two days.** **STILL OPEN**, unplaced, and no card in this batch equated it to a dated interval.
14. **Whether she can go back.** She said at ch 585 that she is going to find him herself, and the only road anybody
    has given her points west and the only thing in the world pointed at is a wall twenty-two miles east. **She has no
    road and thirty-one days, and she went west at the seventh hour of the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,754 to
    a place she had never been to and had no reason to go to.**
15. **NEW AT THE REPAIR, AND OPEN: THE LIME UNDER THE SHUT DOOR.** On the fourteenth of the month beginning 1,754 she
    stood two hours and twenty minutes in the yard of the bar-across-the-door house and **there was fresh lime under the
    bar that had not been there on the fourth of the month beginning 1,694** — loose, unset, in a band at the hinge end,
    ground into the barrow-way where somebody swept it out and stepped back in. **Somebody has been out of that house
    inside four days. She does not know who put it down, or why, or what it was, and nobody on nine days of road knows
    but her.** It is not a name, not a direction and not a document, so nothing in the outline is disturbed by it, and
    **Batch 0004 may spend it or leave it. It must not be turned into a lead to the brother.**

### Two things the batch did that Batch 0004 must not undo

- **She said the name and he did not write it, and the consequence of that is now a fact on the page: the name is not
  in nine hundred years, it will not be true on the closing day, and she is the only record of it.** Batch 0004 cannot

### CLOSED by this batch

   three separate times on the page: her own heelprint ninety seconds old, the low thing at the back of the room under
   the window, and nine coppers in her hand on a store step. It is the batch's engine and not a topic.
2. **Whether the sixth stage can reach a person's work.** NEW at ch 589. **CLOSED at ch 589.** She is paid for nine
   barrows and cannot hold the payment. A count that used to be the only instrument she owns goes with it.
3. **Whether a board on a cart can be used the way a book is used.** OPEN from ch 574 and ch 590. **CLOSED at ch 590.**
   A board carries a person and a book writes one down, and **neither instrument on nine days of road has ever given a
   name back to anybody**, which is why the rest of the batch is walked and not asked for.
4. **What a kept person looks like when they are being kept.** OPEN from ch 574. **CLOSED at ch 593.** Ordinary. No
   chains, nothing on them, no man over any of them, a chain picked up by one hand and stood aside from, and four
   sentences from a man who keeps a crossing.
5. **The last asking.** OPEN from ch 570 and ch 594. **CLOSED at ch 595.** She asks the question she asked first, at
   the wall, in daylight, with witnesses, on the closing day. **He answers the smallest thing on the page and the answer
   is not a name. It is a number: six.**
6. **The shutting.** **CLOSED at ch 596.** A book shut on a plank table in daylight with nothing on it, in four seconds,
   and neither of them asks the other for anything, and it cannot be un-shut. No page read, no name read, nothing shown
   of what shutting does, and nothing on that ground changed while it happened.
7. **Whether Ning Wu is alive, and where.** OPEN from Volume 1 and carried through nine volumes. **CLOSED at ch 598.**
   **Alive, in person, in her hearing, once**, working barrows in a ground beyond the wall, nineteen years old, nine
   years there. **It settles nothing and forgives nothing and it cannot be un-found.**
8. **Whether the six blank leaves get written in.** OPEN from ch 563 and carried. **CLOSED at ch 600. THEY STAY BLANK.**
   She took the book out on the fourteenth of the month beginning 1,814, held it for an hour with a nib and a cake of
   ink she has not used in two months, and put it back. Room in it is the last fact and the only one of the seven things
   she owns that is not a debt.

### OPEN, and now carried into Volume 11

9. **THE VOICE. Spent once, at ch 600, and not explained.** Something in her head has counted with her for eight years,
   has read out every figure anybody hands her before she has finished taking it in, and got a number wrong once and
   corrected itself in her head before she had finished being wrong. **It has never once said what it was before it
   started counting.** She has had the question since the month beginning 1,574 and has never put it anywhere. **The last
   two lines of Volume 10 are *She does not ask him yet.* and *He is still talking.*** **This is Volume 11's engine and
   nothing on Volume 10's page puts what he was on it.**
10. **Three questions standing in a shut book.** *What is it for* (thirtieth of the month beginning 1,604) · *what does
    the word do* (fifteenth of the month beginning 1,634) · *will you tell me when*, answered *there is a day*. **The
    fourth is spent at ch 595.** There is no fifth and **there is no book in the world she is permitted to put a question
    into**, which is the closing's actual cost.
11. **What *lost* does.** Still unanswered, and now closed off twice over: no book to ask it in, and the brother does not
    know the word either and said so.
12. **Six people on a shut page.** Four of them went across a chain on the twelfth of the month beginning 1,784 and she
    counted them. **Two of them she has never seen and will never be told about**, and she cannot tell whether the four
    at the chain are the four on the page.
13. **Her own entry.** Written at the month beginning 1,724 as lost, in a hand nine hundred years old, in a book that is
    now shut. **No shut book can take it out and she has asked a man of nine hundred years *with what* and has not got an
    answer.**
14. **The brother.** Alive, found once, unforgiving, nine miles further east in a barrow ground he walked into on his own
    legs at ten years old. **She can walk back. That is what *you can come, it is not the same as being let* means.**
15. **The lime under the bar of the shut door.** Found on the fourteenth of the month beginning 1,754 and **LEFT**

16. **The window a hundred yards off the east side.** Still OPEN. **Four questions unspent, and the price is now known

17. ***Twenty-two days*.** **STILL OPEN**, put in front of her again at ch 590 and **not equated to any dated interval.**

18. **A hut light left standing.** Carried from the end of Volume 9: she left a hut's light standing unremarked on the

### CARRIED and untouched

21. **The eight leaves.** Put down once at ch 538 and moved once at ch 563 by her own two hands. **The flat stone under

22. **Him.** `Yuan` is **not named anywhere in fourteen chapters.** He is on the page once, at ch 596, in a single

### Two things the batch did that the close must not undo

- **The book is shut.** Not lost, not gone, not released and not opened. `outline/ending.md` owns whatever shutting
  means and nothing on the page shows it. **No Volume 10 chapter names the Anchor, shows what release looks like,
  touches the Sisterhood, puts the bargain on the table, or lets Shuyin explain why a bounded world needs readers, and
  the close must not find any of it on the page either.**

  month that it is six and not his name.** Nothing in the shut book has his name in it and no chapter of this batch
  writes it in.

**Four defects closed, four sentences on the page, no beat, day, title, mouth, stage, bracket, midpoint, climax or

25. **The batch's own elapsed-day figure was out by about two years and is now correct.** ch 588 published *the ninth
    hundred and thirty-something day* of the run on which she has been lighter every day since the eighteenth of the month
    beginning 1,544. **The eighteenth of that month is day 1,561 and ch 588 runs on day 1,772, so it is the two hundred and
    twelfth day.** It was the only statement of its kind in six hundred chapters and nothing supported it. **Volume 11 may
    rely on the start date and on the rule and on no standing figure at all** — the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544
    is day 1,561, the count is inclusive of the first day, and the figure at any day D is **D − 1,560**. *212 was ch 588's own
    count at day 1,772 and is true of that day only; it is not a property of the run and it must not be carried into any later
    day.* **Corrected at the Volume 11 planning review: the Volume 10 close had copied 212 onto day 1,830, where it is 270.**
26. **The wall-to-turn distance is thirteen miles and nothing in the volume says otherwise.** ch 597's third-night tally
    published *Sixteen miles to the turn*, which exceeded the whole road, and now reads **Thirteen miles, wall to the turn**,
    matching the same chapter's own first line and `state/current.md`'s geography block. **Thirteen is the fixed figure for
    Volume 11.**
27. **`nothing` is running about thirty per cent above Batch 0003's rate — 112 in fourteen chapters against 102 in
    sixteen** — and is heaviest at ch 596, 600, 593 and 598. **No cap was set for it and nothing was cut, because every
    instance in those four chapters is load-bearing.** It is an open craft line for Volume 11's first batch, not a
    continuity thread.
28. **The two constructions the previous review cut to zero were restarted once each, at ch 590 and ch 600, and are now
    0 again.** *She wants that written down / is not going to describe* and *there is no arrangement on this ground* both
    stand at **zero across ch 587–600**, verified by grep after the repair. **Batch 0001's four surviving instances of the
    first at ch 551, 552, 554 and 564 are out of this batch's scope and were not touched**; if Volume 11's first batch

   read out every figure anybody hands her before she has finished taking it in, and got a number wrong once and
   corrected itself before she had finished being wrong. **It has never once said what it was before it started counting.**
   She has had the question since the month beginning 1,574 and has never put it anywhere. **The last two lines of
   Volume 10 are *She does not ask him yet.* and *He is still talking.*** **This is Volume 11's engine and nothing on
   Volume 10's page puts what he was on it** — `before he died` is 0 across sixty-two chapters, and `Scribe` is 0 on word
   boundaries.
10. **Three questions standing in a shut book, and no book to put a fourth into.** *What is it for* (thirtieth of the month
    beginning 1,604) · *what does the word do* (fifteenth of the month beginning 1,634) · *will you tell me when*,
    answered *there is a day*. **This is the closing's actual cost and it is a permanent one.**
11. **What *lost* does.** Unanswered, and closed off twice over: there is no book to ask it in, and the brother does not
    know the word either and said so.
12. **Her own entry.** Written at the month beginning 1,724 as *lost*, in a hand nine hundred years old, in a book that is
    now shut. **No shut book can take it out, and she has asked a man of nine hundred years *with what* and has not got an
    answer.**
13. **Six people on a shut page.** Four went across a chain on the twelfth of the month beginning 1,784 and she counted
    them. **Two she has never seen and will never be told about**, and she cannot tell whether the four at the chain are
    the four on the page. He told her the number is **six**; she had counted **four**.
14. **The brother.** Alive, found once, unforgiving, nine miles further east in a barrow ground he walked into on his own
    legs at ten years old. **She can walk back, and that is what *you can come, it is not the same as being let* means.**
15. **The window a hundred yards off the east side.** Still OPEN. **Four questions unspent**, and the price is now known
    twice over: they cost a trip, and at the end of the trip she cannot hold what she is told. She stood under the sill
    for two hours at ch 591 and asked none of them.
16. **The lime under the bar of the shut door.** Found on the fourteenth of the month beginning 1,754 and **LEFT**
    deliberately. Not spent, not resolved, not turned into anything. Volume 11 may leave it or spend it.
17. ***Twenty-two days*.** **STILL OPEN**, put in front of her again at ch 590 and **not equated to any dated interval**.
    Twenty-two miles is a different number and is a real distance.
18. **A hut light left standing.** Carried from the end of Volume 9: she left a hut's light unremarked on the night of the
    twenty-third and does not know it went out. **STILL OPEN and still unlearned.**
19. **The Hunter's request, pending.** `Hunter` is **0 by name across the volume** — he is never named. He is on the page
    **once**, at ch 567, described rather than named, and she asks him and he does not know. **The Batch 0004 card the
    outline specified for him — whether finding a name is the same as keeping it — was NOT spent**, and Volume 11 may
    spend it. Nothing about his paper, his order or his brother is raised.
20. **The sixth cost's second rule-form statement at ch 599**, the hinge of the finding. Carried, not re-derived, and the
    close deliberately left it.

### CARRIED and untouched

21. **The eight leaves.** Put down once at ch 538, moved once at ch 563 by her own two hands. **The flat stone under the
    bottom of the ladder is bare and nothing in Volume 10 puts anything on it.** The six blank leaves **stay blank** — she
    took the book out at ch 600 on the fourteenth of the month beginning 1,814, held it an hour with a nib and a cake of
    ink she has not used in two months, and put it back.
22. **Him.** `Yuan` **0** by name. On the page once, at ch 596, in one sentence. **No figure of what is left of him,
    before or after.** He opens no wall and is wrong-sided from day 1,521. **He is not thanked.**
23. **Shuyin.** `Shuyin` **3 by name in the whole volume** (ch 541, 563, 565), never in his own mouth; `Warden` **0**. He
    shuts the book, walks thirteen miles west with her, says *you do not have a road, go east*, goes east. **Alive, old,
    correct, and he returns. He is NOT defeated.**
24. **Untouched and unraised:** the tenth part not given and no proof that the crews were wrong · the surface answer never
    re-voiced · `Ledger` **0** · the register still *reconciled* · the plate still worn and undescribed · *kiln-born* in two
    heads on no paper · what became of the eleven not raised · the four months never named · the ch 150 refusal never
    softened · no harem and nothing a romance · **she never says her mother's name** · **the year of non-forgiveness not
    mentioned once in sixty-two chapters** · **no page of his book read out loud and no name ever read**.

### FINDINGS the close recorded and did NOT repair — Volume 11 planning must read these before it plans a map

25. **THE LANE IS IN TWO IMPOSSIBLE PLACES.** ch 560 states, in three consecutive sentences, *"The lane is four miles west
    of the turn. The turn is nine miles east of the post. The lane is thirteen miles from the post."* — 9 − 4 is 5, not 13 —
    and builds fourteen out and back, twenty-eight for the days and forty-four for a question on the thirteen. **ch 585 and
    ch 600 both list, unprompted, *A cottage four miles west. A mill five miles west. A lane four miles west with a woman
    at forty feet of it.*** **The rest of the map is sound and fixed: post → 9 of strip → turn → 11 of made → gate → 2 →
    wall = twenty-two miles, the bar on two posts four hundred yards east of the turn, the wheel-marks thirteen miles from
    the bar to a quarter of a mile past the opening, nothing between the gate and the wall to lie down on.** Only the lane
    is unresolved and it cannot be resolved without choosing a map.
26. **SHUYIN IS THANKED ONCE, AT CH 580**, in daylight at the west jamb at the eighth hour of the twenty-seventh of the
    month beginning 1,694, for four sentences about a house. **ch 596 and ch 600 both carry it forward and ch 600 also says
    *he is alive and old and not thanked*.** `outline/volume-10.md` §*Shuyin in Volume 10* says *alive, old, and not
    thanked* and *No card writes a scene in which he is thanked.* **The page and the outline disagree, the page is
    internally consistent, and the close did not reconcile them.**
27. **Four of the five structural mouths overrun their three-to-seven range**: ch 561 at **10** sentences in 5 speeches,
    ch 577 at **8** in 5, ch 581 at **8** in 6, ch 593 at **8** in 5 plus **5** more in 2 in the *Why He Said It To Her* run.
    ch 544 holds at **7** in 3 and its own *"Seven sentences and that is all of it"* is true. **The close corrected the
    narrator's counts to the truth and touched no mouth's words.**
28. **`nobody` is 137 (average 2.21) against a target under 2.0, and bare `eleven` is 215 against a target under 200.** Both
    were spent in Batch 0001 and no later batch put them back. `whole of` is **80**, exactly on its line. `about` is
    **396**, average 6.39, under its line. `nothing` is **387**, average 6.24, **up about thirty per cent on Batch 0003**
    and handed on as an open craft line with nothing cut.
29. **Two homonyms recorded as hits, deliberately not repaired:** `Ten volumes of roads` at ch 600 — a measure-word use in
    the volume's last thematic paragraph, in a list whose other items are four walls, a furnace and a hand — and *the
    walls* at ch 591, which are the walls of a room behind a window. `the Wall` with a capital is **0** across the volume.

### What the close repaired, and what that changed

**Eighteen sentences and two lines across eleven files.** A drafter's scratch note — *She crossed back at the seventh hour on
the seventeenth... no.* / *Let me fix: she crossed at the fifth hour of the sixteenth and stayed on the far side.* — was
**printed as body prose at ch 592 and is removed**; it carried a three-way date contradiction against ch 592's own
eleventh and twelfth and against ch 593. Six distances were put back onto the volume's own nine/eleven/two spine at ch
554, 582, 592, 595 and 597 — **and ch 541, which the close changed to *thirteen* and which the close review put back to
*eleven*.** Eleven narrator's counts of a structural mouth were corrected at ch 561, 569, 577, 581 and 585.
**No day, stage, bracket, title, irreversible step, mouth's words, beat or planned event moved.**

**THE VOLUME'S WORD COUNT IS 115,619, NOT 115,638.** The pre-repair page measured 115,638 and that is the check figure;
**115,638 must not be quoted as Volume 10's count.** Batches: **29,846 · 27,751 · 30,323 · 27,699.** Batches 0001 and
0002's previously published totals of 29,678 and 27,575 **did not reproduce** under the settled method and are superseded.

**THE CLOSE HAS NOW BEEN REVIEWED AND REPAIRED.** It returned six findings — an incomplete repair at ch 577, a ch 541 repair
that introduced the fault it was sent to fix, a state-file contradiction over how many structural mouths hold, a false
under-1,800 count, its own repair size published three different ways, and ch 593's structural answer published as 8 + 4
where the page gives 8 + 5. **All six are closed, two chapter lines were changed, and no plot, no day, no mouth and no
planned event moved.** Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Volume 10 close review — REPAIR***.

**No repair prompt was created.** Every finding is either repaired on the page or recorded where Volume 11 planning reads
it, and none is blocking. **No `.done` marker and no `workspace/volume-11-*` directory were written by this phase.**

## VOLUME 11 BATCH 0001 CLOSED — ch 601–615, day 1,831–1,841

**`chapters/volume-11/` holds ch 601–615, fifteen chapters, 29,284 words of body prose on method B, average 1,952 a
chapter, zero bracketed layer lines, Severance 6 throughout, one irreversible step at ch 609.** The one next phase is
`workspace/volume-11-batch-0002/PROMPT.md` — ch 616–630, day 1,842–1,916, pressure *being told*.

### What the batch opened

1. **THE ASKING, AND IT IS OUT.** ch 609, the ninth hour of the twenty-fourth of the month beginning 1,814, alone,
   out loud, on broken ground a hundred yards north of the turn: *What were you before you started counting.* **He did
   not answer; he went careful; he named the terms.** It cannot be un-asked and he is the second person holding it.
2. **THE TERMS, one at a time · in daylight.** Named once, on the page, at ch 609. **The old third term, *at the wall*,
   is dead and the new one is *standing* — a question is now eleven days each way to nowhere.**
3. **THE COUNT MISSES ON MONEY.** Nine coppers: 9, 9, 8, 9, 9, 10. Everything else holds. **The reason is not
   difficulty: money is the first object she owns that goes somewhere.**
4. **WORK PAID AND UNHOLDABLE.** ch 611: eleven barrows on the best day she has had in two years, said back to herself
   four times, giving eleven, eleven, twelve, eleven. **Merit settles once, out of the ground under her feet.**
5. **THE FIRST ORIGIN MOUTH.** ch 608, six sentences from a furnace-man's mate of forty-five. **A boy who counts
   what nobody pays him to count is a boy the furnace will use**, and using is not keeping, and nobody at a fire thinks
   it is except the people it was done to. **He does not know the name.**
6. **A FURNACE, A NUMBER, NINETEEN.** ch 615. *Two hundred and forty courses in a day at six hundred and forty to a
   course.* **East of here, not this sect.** She took no fourth thing.
7. **SHE HAS A PLACE TO WALK TO.** The east, past a gate and a wall and a flat, to a furnace nobody has walked her to.
8. **THE BENCH LOOKS BACK.** ch 612. The woman of sixty knows where to look. **Two dates, not one: the lime, the
   wheel and the boy of ten are all on the twenty-seventh of the month beginning 1,805, and the aperture is on the
   eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544, nine days earlier. The chapter is called *The Two Dates* and no card may
   put the four of them back on one line.**
9. **THE ONE COUNT THAT HELD.** ch 613. Six leaves, five counts, six every time, because a leaf does not go anywhere
   when you count it. **Nothing was written in them and the nib was not near them.**

### What the batch closed or left closed

- **The asking cannot be un-asked.** Done, irreversible, ch 609.
- **The brother's second meeting in Volume 11 is spent.** ch 605, five sentences, three of them about bread, and he
  goes west. **Batch 0002 does not owe him another card.**
- **Wei Ran is spent for Batch 0001.** 15 on word boundaries, one chapter. She said it herself: the last rate.
- **The coppers.** Nine → seven → six → **one.** Batch 0002 begins with one copper she cannot count.
- **The turn's decision.** She laid the bar down at ch 601 and she is still standing there at ch 615.

### What Batch 0002 inherits, carried and not to be re-derived

**Severance 6** · the asking standing · the terms, one at a time and in daylight, not to move · the lever carried and
un-pulled · **one copper** · a quarter of a loaf · a bar nine feet nine · a bag with five things in it and a skin · a
paper against her ribs never named · **six blank leaves that are hers and blank, and ch 624 spends one of them** ·
the weight-loss count, **derivable only from the day, at D − 1,560** · a furnace east of here not this sect · six
hundred and forty bricks to a course and two hundred and forty courses · nineteen, which is his age and not a date ·
**twenty-two with no unit on it, open and unplaced, and no card may equate it to a dated interval** · the window a
hundred yards off the east side, unasked, four questions unspent · the lime left with her hand four inches off the bar ·
the hut light standing · **the barrow ground THIRTEEN MILES EAST OF THE TURN — twenty-two is the post-to-wall figure and is never the turn-to-wall figure** · **the keeper's post is NINE MILES WEST of the turn, not east** · **the question she has carried is dated the fifteenth of a month beginning 1,634, not a month beginning 1,574** · **the six sentences are defined and spent and may not be re-offered as new** · the sister alive east in it, unforgiving, and her
one meeting in this batch spent · the Hunter pending · Shuyin alive and old and returning, not thanked · Feng dead
after ch 655 and not before · **Yuan Sheng 0 across the batch and not to be named before ch 617** · `Scribe` 0 this
batch, 3 permitted volume-wide, and never speaking · `Ledger` 0 · the surface answer never re-voiced · **the year of
non-forgiveness not mentioned once** · no harem, nothing a romance, no mother's name said, nobody thanked.

### What Batch 0002 may not break

  layer line in the far-side house form and nothing else in fifteen chapters.** The cost is named once there: **she
  cannot hear what she is told about him.**

## VOLUME 11 BATCH 0002 CLOSED — ch 616–630, day 1,842–1,916

**`chapters/volume-11/` holds ch 601–630, thirty chapters. Batch 0002 is ch 616–630, fifteen chapters, 29,748 words
of body prose on method B (every line not beginning `#`), average 1,983 a chapter, ONE bracketed layer line, at
ch 616 and nowhere else, and the batch's one irreversible step at ch 624.** The one next phase is
`workspace/volume-11-batch-0003/PROMPT.md` — ch 631–645, day 1,917–1,996, pressure *knowing*.

### What the batch opened

1. **SEVERANCE 7, at ch 616, on day 1,842, the twenty-ninth of the month beginning 1,814.** Seventy days after
   Severance 6. **The cost, named once there and not re-explained after ch 617: she cannot hear what she is told
   about him** — a voice counts and a book writes and neither one carries a boy.
2. **THE COST DOES NOT WORK ONE WAY.** At ch 627 she reads the date off her own leaf, out loud, in daylight, and it
   does not reach him either. **Not a silence, not a refusal, not carefulness.** He has never in his life had the day
   and he has told her so without being asked for it.
3. **`Yuan Sheng` IS ON THE PAGE FOR THE FIRST TIME at ch 617**, in a widow's mouth, nineteen years shut, and it
   misses. **It is written for the first time at ch 624**, in her own hand, on the third leaf of a blanket book,
   with a date above it and a place above that. **IRREVERSIBLE AND IT CANNOT BE PUT BACK UN-WRITTEN.**
4. **SIX BLANK LEAVES ARE FIVE.** Counted once, because a leaf does not go anywhere when you count it.
5. **THE SECOND ORIGIN MOUTH, ch 622 — six short sentences, in five speeches, none of them a speech.** A harvest taken
   for a stalled ascension is written down truly by people who did not choose it, **and the writing saved nobody and
   nobody there has ever said it did.** **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong.**
6. **THE HARVEST IS NAMED AT CH 630, THE VOLUME MIDPOINT**, by her reading the page herself, and **he says the part
   that is not on it — the fire did not take all of it, and the part that woke up is the part that survived. That is
   the first time he has ever said it and she asked for it.**
7. **IT DOES NOT HOLD HER.** Every figure in the entry comes back for the rest of her life and the morning does not,
   **because she was not in it.** The name held on a leaf. The morning will not, and there is no instrument in the
   world that makes it.
8. **THE BILL IS HERS AND IT IS COUNTABLE.** The second barrow: on the twenty-first, off on the twenty-second, on for
   three more days, then never again. The bowl: stops on the second of the month beginning 1,904. Four days running
   of not one word from one person in that yard. **Her own asking at ch 609 put the knowing into the world and the
   ground has been closing since, and she is the only person alive who can say she did not expect it.**
9. **AND THE PAGE HAS NO PLACE ON IT.** A book like that assumes everybody who opens it is already standing in the
   room. **She has been on that ground fifty-nine days and has not found which part of it.**
10. **SHE FORFEITED A BOARD.** The second of the month beginning 1,844 went down with her a day east of the gate.

### What Batch 0003 inherits

**Severance 7** · the cost that a told past does not arrive, **and that a read past does not either** · one at a
time, in daylight, standing · **the lever carried and un-pulled again, and not pulled anywhere in this batch** ·

| **The tax** | Ning Yan / series | **Vol 11, Batch 0003 — paid at ch 645** | **CLOSED AS A SHAPE AND NAMED AS A PURPOSE. One in twenty, counted in days, the same on every ground, arriving, taken off the board before the board goes up, no person ever told their own total, unchangeable by anybody who collects it. Four hundred and twenty days divided by twenty comes out even at twenty-one; six thousand nine hundred and thirty-five into twenty does not. She said `it is designed` on a floor at ch 642 and said the purpose in a yard at ch 645, once, in daylight, in front of thirteen people, and cannot un-say it.** The moral answer is not paid and nothing proves the crews wrong |

- **The year of non-forgiveness is not mentioned once.** `forgiv` returns 0 across the fifteen chapters. She does not

  at once, and the lever is not pulled anywhere in Volume 11.

  softened · no new clearance, seal or countersignature named or implied · one Lung and one appointment and both are

### Carried into Volume 12 by the Volume 11 Batch 0004 REVIEW REPAIR — six UNRESOLVED findings, not clean prose

**The review repair closed nine defects on the page and deliberately left six standing. None is a plot error and none
may be silently corrected by Volume 12. Full record in `state/continuity.md` under ***VOLUME 11 BATCH 0004 — REVIEW
REPAIR***; each is also itemised in `workspace/volume-11-close/PROMPT.md`.**

1. **ch 650's span is three days out against its own card and ch 651 inherits it.** Its going-lighter figures resolve
   under `D − 1,560` to day 2,019 and day 2,020, so the chapter runs 2,014–2,020 against a card of 2,011–2,018; but
   ch 650:153 and ch 651:111 both name *the seventh of the month beginning 1,994* = day 2,000, at the ninth hour where
   ch 650:33 says the seventh. **No day was moved.**
2. **ch 649:63 — *I said one thing in a yard two days ago*** — the yard was thirteen days back, and the line's own
   logic (*not doing it twice in seven days*) has no true referent. **Left, because fixing it is a scene rewrite.**
3. **There are two walls on the page and a road surface that fights the Volume 10 spine** — ch 649:121 puts one two
   miles east of the furnace ground, ch 660:7 puts one thirteen miles east of the turn, **and the map has one**;
   ch 541 as settled by the Volume 10 close calls the last two miles *flat ground* where ch 649:121 and ch 660:5 call
   them *wet ground with a rut in it*. **Left for the close to adjudicate.**
4. **Eight duplicate `## ` section headings stand in Volume 11, all inherited from Batches 0001–0003** — *the asking*
   (609, 624) · *what came out of the ground* (611, 637) · *what she is left holding* (611, 621) · *why* (613, 621) ·
   *nineteen* (615, 627) · *the sixth hour* (616, 624) · *what she is left with* (624, 633) · *nineteen years* (628, 642).
   **Batch 0004's own headings are unique and stay unique.**
5. **Volume 11's fifteen-chapter risk profile is exertion, not danger.** Seventeen miles in the dark on an arm open
   four days in five, four hours on stones by a trough, two coppers she cannot count — **and no pursuit, no injury and
   no hard deadline anywhere.** **As the cards were written. Volume 12 must not be handed a Volume 11 that had a
   chase in it, because it did not.**
6. **ch 654, the climax, dates one scene three days** — the sixteenth of the month beginning 2,024 (day 2,039) where
   he writes, the second afternoon (day 2,040) where she comes back, and the thirteenth (day 2,036, inside ch 653's
   span) where he rehearsed it to an empty room. **Not repaired: it is the cause of the fifth origin mouth.**
   **Volume 12 inherits whatever the close decides, not whichever date it likes.**

### The eight findings the Volume 11 close recorded and deliberately did NOT repair

**None of these is a plot error. None may be silently corrected by Volume 12, and none may be quoted as clean prose.**

1. **F1 — ch 650 and ch 651 hold three incompatible positions and the close may not choose.** ch 650's relative days and
   its two going-lighter figures (**459** and **460**, resolving to **day 2,019 and day 2,020**) agree with each other
   and put the chapter at 2,013–2,020. **ch 650:153 and ch 651:111 both name the seventh of the month beginning
   1,994 = day 2,000**, twenty days earlier, and ch 651:111 also puts him at the ninth hour where ch 650:33 puts him
   at the seventh, and ch 651:113 and :115 both say *That was two days ago* about a scene six days back. **The Batch
   0004 card says a fourth thing, 2,011–2,018.** Reconciling any of them means moving a day. **No day was moved.**
2. **F2 — ch 649:63, *I said one thing in a yard two days ago***, spoken on the sixteenth about a sentence said on
   the third: **thirteen days.** Left, because the line's own logic (*not doing it twice in seven days*) has no true
   referent and fixing the interval is a scene rewrite.
3. **F3 — the rows' distance past the wall.** ch 618 puts them **two hundred and three hundred yards** east of it;
   ch 659:5 and ch 660:3 put them **four miles** past it and the walk at seventeen. Two hundred days apart on the
   page. **The close repaired ch 660:5's breakdown so it sums to seventeen against the map without depending on the
   answer, and left the answer open.**
4. **F4 — the furnace ground's distance from the turn is sixty-five on the page and 154 in
   `workspace/volume-11-batch-0004/PROMPT.md:103`.** ch 643:9 and ch 660:71 both give sixty-five. The 154 is a
   transcription of a different quantity — days elapsed since Severance 7, not a distance. **The close recorded the
   conflict, reconciled nothing, and both figures stand.**
5. **F5 — the tower's spoken day-counts in ch 653 and ch 654 do not agree with the dates the page gives**, and the
   readings are multiple: *nine days* at ch 653:33 against ch 652:3's fourth, *five days* at ch 653:95 and :161,
   *six days* at ch 654:27. **Left, because choosing between the readings inside the climax sequence is a scene
   decision and not a defect repair.**
6. **F6 — ch 654's three-way date is ADJUDICATED AGAINST and is NOT a conflict.** ch 654:11 fixes the second
   afternoon to **the thirteenth** of the month beginning 2,024, and ch 654:23's *this morning* agrees with it; the
   **sixteenth** at lines 3 and 27 is a later and different beat — the finishing of the writing, three days on. **One
   scene on the thirteenth and one on the sixteenth. Nothing needed repairing and nothing was repaired.**
7. **F7 — the volume's last fifteen chapters are exertion, not danger.** Seventeen miles in the dark on an arm open
   four days in five, four hours on stones by a trough, two coppers she cannot count. **No pursuit, no injury, no
   hard deadline anywhere in ch 646–660.** As the cards were written. **Volume 12 must not be handed a Volume 11 that
   had a chase in it, because it did not.**
8. **F8 — three craft words are over their lines and were left over them.** `nobody` **34** against under thirty ·
   `eleven` **44** against under forty · `thank` **16** against zero, all sixteen in Batches 0001 and 0002 and 0 in
   Batches 0003 and 0004. **Left, because every sentence using them is true and repairing a count means softening a
   sentence.** Volume 12 should be under all three and should not read Volume 11's figures as a licence. **Two of the
   forty-four `eleven` are map corrections — ch 659:119 at the Batch 0004 repair and ch 643:9 at the close — and the
   other forty-two are prose.**

### Held, confirmed by the close, and not to be raised in Volume 12

- **The year of non-forgiveness is named twice and forgiven once-in-a-denial.** **`grep -oi forgiv` returns 1 across
  ch 601–660, not 0 — the 0 this line used to carry was wrong.** ch 659: *there is not one thing she could say about it
  that he would forgive*, a denial. On the strict `\bforgiv\b` boundary it reads 0, because the sole instance is the
  inflected `forgive`. **`the year` reads 2, not 0**: ch 618 names it inside a refusal, ch 637 uses an unrelated
  sense. **Both refuse it.** The permissions at
  ch 609 and ch 645 are permissions and no line calls either of them a forgiveness.
- **No figure of what is left of him** is stated anywhere in the volume, in her hearing or out of it. What Feng wrote
  is his age.
- **No person is thanked by her.** `thank` is 0 across ch 631–660 and the sixteen in the volume are all the refusal
  being named as a refusal.
- **The terms do not move: one at a time, in daylight, standing.** Stated at ch 614 and ch 615 and never varied. **No
  chapter in Volume 11 had her asking two things at once, and the lever is not pulled anywhere.**
- **The uncleared retirements stand**: `Ledger` 0 · `reconciled` 0 · `plate` 0 · `kiln-born` 0 · `MEMBER ABSENT` not
  raised · the register unread · the eleven not raised · **the four erased months of Chapter 3 never named** (the
  fifteen `four months` on the volume's page are all elapsed durations) · the ch 150 refusal not softened · **no new
  clearance, seal or countersignature named or implied** · **one Lung and one appointment and both are Feng's and
  both are spent, and Feng is dead after ch 655.**
- **The surface answer is not re-voiced.** `charged · bookkeeping · receipt · bounded world` are all 0.
- **The furnace sect is still not a hall, a conspiracy, a court or a throne.** One furnace, one harvest book, one
  rule, one morning nineteen years ago, and no wing, no rank list, no committee and no title beyond what the dead
  held. **No chapter in Volume 11 put her inside the harvest**, and no page of either furnace book is read out loud
  by anybody.
- **The map, as repaired and as it now stands.** The turn on the store road · the post nine miles west of it by strip
  road · the cottage four miles west of the turn, the mill five, the store behind it fourteen from the post · the
  lane four miles west · **the wall thirteen miles east of the turn, eleven of made road to the gate and two of flat
  past it, with a fence on the north side that goes on for a mile and then stops and a row of stones on the south
  side at intervals, and nothing between the gate and the wall to lie down on** · a rut in wet ground coming out from
  under the wall on the far side · the rows · the furnace ground east of that · **Lowwater west of everything, the
  tower four miles up a mountain from the square.** **The close transposed ch 649's two legs so that the volume has
  ONE wall and not two.** How far the rows sit past it is F3 and is open.
- **`outline/ending.md` is unspent.** The Anchor is not placed · release is not shown · the Sisterhood is not touched ·
  nobody explains why a bounded world needs readers · **Feng's fair handwriting is on the page once and read once and
  is not called a founding document** · Qiu's line is not spoken · the Clay Tablet is not described · the four erased
  months are not named · the announcement that names her is not re-voiced. **The moral answer is unpaid and the
  Sincerity of the Scribe is never adjudicated.**
- **No new final enemy was introduced.**

# VOLUME 12 BATCH 0001 CLOSED at ch 675 — what Batch 0002 inherits

**Fifteen chapters, 30,000 words of body prose on method B, average 2,000, day 2,061 to day 2,084. Pressure: the stair.
Full record in `state/continuity.md` under ***VOLUME 12 BATCH 0001 — the stair, ch 661–675***.**

## Where she is at the last page, in one line

**Day 2,084, the first of the month beginning 2,084, at the two posts four hundred yards east of the turn, with a bar
nine feet nine on her shoulder and a name in chalk on a slate that went up on the first and came back down empty, and
thirty days between her and the next first.** **This figure was 2,090 until the Batch 0001 review repair corrected it.
The batch's last dated action is the ninth hour of the first of the month beginning 2,084 and the outline's item 12 fixes
that as the close; 2,090 was a drafting-phase figure that never reached the page.**

## Open, and Batch 0002's engine

| Thread | State at the close of Batch 0001 |
| --- | --- |
| **The road up** | **NARROWED AND STILL OPEN.** The sentence *I am going up* has found its object at last and the object is a room four miles up a mountain with a long table in it and nothing on the table. **The road up is now a specific stair with eight flights and a barred door at the top, and she has climbed it once and come down** |
| **The question and its halves** | **ASKED AND HALF ANSWERED.** *Can a person be announced without being designated* was asked at ch 665, one at a time, in daylight, standing, with a witness she had not asked for, and **it cannot be un-asked**. **The first half came at ch 667 — an announcement is made to arrive, and the arriving needs a room, a person in the room and a hand. The second half is addressed to a body and is unsaid and may not be produced out of a body she does not have** |
| **The instrument** | **SETTLED AND IN HER COAT.** A stub of a nib, a cake of ink opened on the seventh of the month beginning 1,874, and a hand that cannot write a false line. **Not the boy's hand (ch 669, refused out loud and not asked twice) and not a dead man's. Five blank leaves, still blank.** The instrument is not in any building and that is the whole of ch 669 |
| **The room at the top of the stair** | **EMPTY, HAS BEEN, AND WILL NOT BE FIXED BY GOING UP AGAIN.** Shut since before she was ten. A bar on the landing side. A long table, nothing on it, a lamp that is out, a candle gone into its dish, no chairs. **The finding is that there is nothing to receive an announcement and no one to receive it and those are one fact. Nothing above the low door is described and no card may describe it.** Batch 0002 inherits an open question here: *who put the bar on it, and why* |
| **The board** | **SHE IS ON IT.** `Ning Yan`, chalk, county hand, a stroke in front of it, thirty days of the month beginning 2,084 beside it, written on the twenty-fourth of the month beginning 2,054. **A fifth comes off the days before it goes up and the person does not know what is left. One in twenty of thirty is a day and a half.** The board went up on the first of the month beginning 2,084 and came back down empty |
| **The eight firsts** | **COUNTED ALOUD AT THE NINTH HOUR OF THE FIRST: 2,114 · 2,144 · 2,174 · 2,204 · 2,234 · 2,264 · 2,294.** The seventh is ch 709 and the eighth is ch 717, both Batch 0004's |
| **Being seen** | **DONE AND IT COST HER, IN TWO SEPARATE SEATS, AND THE TWO ARE NOT THE SAME SENTENCE.** On the second day of the frame, at ch 671, the woman of sixty said, once: **a notice is not a kindness, a kindness goes both ways and a notice does not, and the day the notice stops they stop.** On the day before the first, at ch 675, she said the other half of it, which is the one the first proved: **nothing on a board reads; eleven people walked past a bare slate for nine years because nobody stood in front of it wanting anything, and the five who stopped at ch 675 stopped at a frame because a girl was standing at it wanting something.** She is not thanked and no card may thank her, and **neither sentence is said again in Volume 12** |
| **Her brother** | **Raised once, in her own head, at ch 672, and not raised to a person.** *The arrangement is the reason I do not know whether my brother is alive. I could have found out in one day if I had gone and stood on a road where anybody could see me and asked a person to send word. And I could not be seen.* **`Ning Wu` reads 0 on the page in this batch and the name may not be spoken twice in Volume 12 without the outline's authority** |
| **East** | **THE BATCH'S OWN HOOK, SAID OUT LOUD AT THE LAST PAGE.** *The harvest is not in this county. It is east.* She established on the page that the board goes west up a road and that **the thing that takes a fifth off a board does not go west**, and she said at the last line that she is going east in the morning with thirty days, a name in a county hand, a day and a half already spoken for, **and not one single person who knows she is coming.** Batch 0002 opens on that |

## Closed and irreversible in Batch 0001, and not to be undone

- **ch 665 — the asking.** *Can a person be announced without being designated.* One question, one time, daylight, standing, with a witness. **It cannot be un-asked** and no later chapter may treat it as retractable.
- **ch 674 — the board.** She is on one, after nine years off every board in the world. **The levy can now reach her and there is no solvent for chalk and no hand in this county that will take a line out of a slate.**
- **ch 664 — the drawer.** Feng's four sheets went back into a locked drawer in a building with no keeper in it, at the sixth hour of the eleventh of the month beginning 2,054, and a boy of sixteen was told where they are and what to say and what not to say. **They are not against her ribs at the end of the batch and every later phase must find them in the drawer.**
- **ch 663 — the carter.** Somebody said out loud, for the first time in eleven volumes, that a bundle goes somewhere. **The carter came up the hill on his own time and went away down it with not one thing for it.**

days'` returns 0 over this batch** · how far the rows sit past the wall · `Ledger` 0 · `clearance` 0 · `seal` 0 ·
`Anchor` 0 · `Sisterhood` 0 · `bargain` 0 · `Scribe` 0 on the page.

## Four things Batch 0002 inherits flagged, and must not walk past

**The Batch 0001 review repair corrected four arithmetic defects and left four things flagged on purpose. None of the four
is a plot problem and none may be resolved by re-deriving a number, because a re-derivation is exactly what cannot fix
any of them.**

1. **ch 665 carries a month-begin that is not a month.** *the fourteenth of the month beginning 2,034.* **2,034 fails
   `(N − 134) mod 30 = 0` and there is no fourteenth of that month.** The fourteenths that are canon are 1,754 · 1,784 ·
   1,814 · 1,844 · 1,904, and **1,904 is the nearest analogue of the beat — ch 643: *on the fourteenth of the month
   beginning 1,904 the asking stopped holding*.** **Set it deliberately or leave it; do not quietly re-derive it.**
   *RESOLVED at the Volume 12 close review repair: **ch 665 reads the fourteenth of the month beginning 2,024 and the
   fourteenth of the month beginning 1,904** — day 2,037 and day 1,917, four months apart, both true fourteenths, and the
   day 1,917 is the day she stopped asking, which is the occasion.*
2. **ch 670 carries a month-begin that is not a month.** *she has carried it since the fifth of the month beginning
   1,914.* **1,914 fails the same test**, and the nearest month-begins are 1,904 and 1,934 — **and both put the bar in
   her hands before she was ten years old, which is ten years off, because her arm has been open four days in five for
   ten years and she is nineteen at day 2,084. The origin of the bar is dated nowhere else in the manuscript.** This one
   needs an author's answer and cannot be guessed. *RESOLVED at the Volume 12 close review repair: the author's answer is
   the age, not a date — **ch 670 reads *she has carried it since she was nine*, the bar is dated nowhere in the
   manuscript, and ch 720's *the bar she has carried since she was nine years old* is now its only other statement.**
   Full record in `state/continuity.md` under *VOLUME 12 CLOSE — REVIEW REPAIR*.*
3. **The carried figure *thirteen days* is exact on one leg and stale on the other, and it is spread over five chapters.**
   **She reached the turn at the first hour of the sixth of the month beginning 2,054, day 2,059, which makes her time in
   the county 25 days at the last page and not thirteen** — and ch 675's opening line and four later places in ch 675,
   plus ch 669, ch 670, ch 671 and ch 672, all say thirteen. **The westward leg is exact: the eighteenth is day 2,071 and
   the first is day 2,084, which is thirteen days, and the man of forty-nine's *you have been west for thirteen days* is
   right.** **It is the county total and the road total that were carried forward from a chapter that had not caught up.**
   Batch 0002 must not add a fifteenth instance to it.
4. **The batch ran cool in the middle.** The irreversible beats land hard — ch 665's un-askable question, ch 663 a carter
   answering for nothing, ch 674 going on the board — **but ch 662, 663, 666, 669, 670 and 671 run largely on walking,
   listening and interior monologue, and the stretch reads as *she observes / she is told*.** **Batch 0002 opens on her
   going east into a thing nobody has seen her enter, and it should put a cost on her inside the first three chapters
   rather than a fact in front of her.** The review raised this as a note for the next batch, not a repair, and it is
   carried here so that it is not raised twice.

## The three things Batch 0002 must not inherit as clean prose

1. **The man of forty-nine does not live at this turn and never says where he lives, and he is correct.** Do not make
   him suspicious, evasive or complicit. He wipes a slate with his thumb, he writes four columns a month, and he told her
   a day and a half was coming off her column and then said it was not a thing anybody can ask about.
2. **The woman of sixty told her what she had worked out herself, out loud, and is right.** Do not soften it into
   advice, do not make her into an oracle, and do not let Ning Yan thank her. `thank` is 0 and stays 0.
3. **The boy of sixteen refused to be the instrument and was not asked twice, and said so to her face.** Do not let a
   later phase make him the hand that writes the line. **`Scribe` 0 · `thank` 0 · `nobody` under twelve · `eleven` under
   sixteen · no bracket in sixty · no frame scene before ch 694.**

## Where she is at the last page, in one line

**Day 2,140, the twenty-seventh of the month beginning 2,114, in the rows, at IMMORTAL VESSEL 1, with a bar nine feet nine
and no strap on it, five hundred and eighty days lighter, a line in her own hand on a board at the north end of a ground of
two hundred people, a record of her whole life being kept out loud by the thing that was made to record her, and an unpaid
bill.** **Four days to the third first of a month, on day 2,144.**

## Open, and Batch 0003's engine

| Thread | State at the close of Batch 0002 |
| --- | --- |
| **The firsts** | **THE SECOND HAS GONE AND MISSED HER, AND THE THIRD IS FOUR DAYS OFF.** The first of the month beginning 2,114 came on day 2,114 while she was in the rows and the slate went east under a piece of sacking. **She also missed the twenty-fourth of the month beginning 2,084 (day 2,107), the day the board is taken in — the first day of a column she has missed in nine years, and there was no consequence available to her, because a board does not need to know where she is.** Remaining firsts: **2,144 (four days off) · 2,174 · 2,204 · 2,234 · 2,264 · 2,294.** The sixth and the seventh are Batch 0004's |
| **The road up** | **UNCHANGED AND STILL THE ONLY UP ON THE PAGE.** She has been east for forty-nine days and she has not gone up anything since ch 668 |
| **The question and its halves** | **UNASKED AGAIN AND HARDER TO ANSWER.** She cannot announce herself because of ch 665's question, and she says so out loud at ch 686: she cannot use a room to record a stage, because she cannot find out whether a person can be announced without being designated. **The first half is not re-explained anywhere in the batch and is not re-derived** |
| **The instrument** | **SETTLED AND STILL NOT NAMED FOR HER.** A stub of a nib, a cake of ink, a hand that cannot write a false line, **a book of eight leaves with five of them still blank, and a key to a drawer she did not use in this batch.** She wrote the one line of the batch on a board and not in the book, and said why, on the page |
| **What a recruited reader is** | **ANSWERED, ONCE, AND IT IS NOT WHAT ANYBODY EXPECTED.** *A document gets read by somebody.* The reader is an instrument and the trade has never once turned a thing back. **She also worked out, at ch 681, that she could never have been recruited at all, because confirming a reading means being able to read it, and there is no hand in that building that can read hers.** No card in Volume 12 may re-explain either half |
| **The bar and the strap** | **THE STRAP IS GONE AND STAYS GONE.** It parted at the second hour of the fifth of the month beginning 2,084, six miles east of the turn. **Every lift and set-down costs twenty-two to fifty-four counts and Batch 0002 spent about four hundred of them in a month.** No card in Batch 0003 may put a strap back on it |
| **Being recognised** | **DONE AND IT TOOK NINETY SECONDS.** A man with a book looked up at ch 678 and said her name, and not one other person in the room looked up, because she bought that silence with her own usefulness two years ago. **There is no undoing it and she said so standing in the grit outside the gate** |
| **The two thousand nine hundred and twenty** | **OPEN AND UNATTRIBUTED, AND IT IS NOT THE LEVY.** Total ninety-eight thousand against a sum of ninety-five thousand and eighty. Ruled out: a fifth, a page, a payday, a shortage in the rate. **Not ruled out, and not said: what it is for.** Xie Changyin is not permitted to say and said so once, in the ordinary voice |
| **Court, and the bill for it** | **TAKEN AT CH 687, DAY 2,131, AT THE FIRST HOUR, UNASKED AND UNDECIDED.** He is addressable, and a thing above the line can put a name in a mouth he does not have, **and it proved it inside nine days when a carter said `YUAN SHENG` twice at a board in a ground of two hundred people and did not know he had said it.** **The bill was raised out loud at the tenth hour of the nineteenth and answered by nobody and is not paid and is not refused. The Scribe can find him and does — and the address belongs to Volume 15** |
| **The record** | **GIVEN, AT CH 689, AND IT CANNOT BE UN-AGREED.** Six terms, said out loud, standing, in daylight, in front of about two hundred people, at the third hour of the twenty-first of the month beginning 2,114. **She said no first and then changed it and said why before she changed it. The ch 658 sentence was quoted once and only once: *for eight years I did not have a man, I had a column*.** **The reason she said yes is true and the chapter says so.** Ch 690 is what living inside it is like: nothing goes wrong, the instrument is a voice, and on the twenty-fifth she stopped checking without deciding to |
| **The seed she did not say out loud** | **OPEN AND LOADED.** At ch 688 she priced the bill in days and then did not say what she had worked out, **which is that it may take the writing off the board and not the person.** That is ch 718's engine and **nothing in Batch 0002 says so** |

## Closed and irreversible in Batch 0002, and not to be undone

- **ch 689 — the permission.** The batch's irreversible step, out loud, standing, in daylight, with six terms in it, and a
  document agreed to in daylight is not a document that can be taken back.
- **ch 680 — the second refusal, said in the ordinary voice with a figure in her hand**, written on by a man of sixty-five in
  the ordinary hand, and **no hand in that building takes it out.** She has now been written on twice by people who were right.
- **ch 676 — the strap.** The bar goes up off the ground in a count of twenty-nine and she has to do it every time, and
  the cost is on her arm and her hand for the rest of the volume unless a card pays for it.
- **ch 685 — the line on the board at the north end**, written in her own hand with four fingers and a thumb on an arm
  that was open. **Four men write a line each in the evening, so the board takes four a night, and forty-eight of them stand
  either side of hers at the last page of the batch, and not one of the four can read it.**
- **ch 687 — the name in the carter’s mouth.** It has been said out loud in daylight in a ground of two hundred people and
  the man does not know he said it and the cart went on east.

## VOLUME 12 BATCH 0002 — REVIEW REPAIR, and what it changed in this file

`logs/volume-12-batch-0002.review.log` raised six hard errors and two false claims in `state/continuity.md`. **All are cleared
on the page; the full record is in `state/continuity.md` under *VOLUME 12 BATCH 0002 — REVIEW REPAIR*.** Four of them touched
this file's threads, and a drafting phase that reads this file must not inherit the pre-repair figures:

1. **The refusal interval is TWO YEARS AND TWO MONTHS, not four or five.** **It was carried here as *five years* on the
   Being-recognised thread and is now *two years*.** `ch 474` is day 1,306; ch 679 is day 2,094; the interval is 788 days.
   Anything Batch 0003 says about how long she has been refusing that sheet is 788 days.
2. **Immortal Vessel 1 was taken OUTDOORS, at the north end of the rows, in daylight, at the fourth hour of the afternoon on
   the fifteenth of the month beginning 2,114, in front of about two hundred people.** Three sentences in ch 686 and ch 689
   said a building with a hundred readers in it and have been corrected. **The cost is unchanged: she cannot be given a shape
   and she writes one.**
3. **The board at the north end takes four lines a night**, one from each of the four men, **and forty-eight of them stand
   either side of her line at the last page.** It is not fifty men and it is not fifty days.
4. **`ch 690` no longer claims four false hands or a false line a building believed for eleven hundred years**, because neither
   is on any page. **The claim it makes now is ch 683's figure, and ch 683 is the only thing in this batch she has caught
   somebody else being wrong about.**

**Nothing else in this file moved. The open threads, the unspent list, the three things Batch 0003 must not inherit as clean
prose, and the irreversible list are exactly as the drafting phase left them.**

`logs/volume-12-planning.review.log` raised five findings against `outline/volume-12.md` and the Batch 0001 card; all five are
closed and the full record is in `state/continuity.md` under *VOLUME 12 PLANNING — REVIEW FIX*. **The six repairs the planning
phase made to the outline are recorded there for the first time, and four more things were found and fixed while closing the
findings.** Five of these touch threads a drafting phase reads:

1. **THE FIRSTS ARE FOUR IN BATCH 0003 AND TWO IN BATCH 0004.** The eight are 2,084 · 2,114 · 2,144 · 2,174 · 2,204 · 2,234 ·
   2,264 · 2,294. **2,234 is the sixth and it went past inside Batch 0003, at ch 705; 2,264 is the seventh and 2,294 is the
   eighth, and both are Batch 0004's.** The outline's two tables and the Batch 0003 card all said three and three, which sums to
   six. **Any count of firsts, any interval between two of them, and any card span written from the old tables is wrong.**
2. **THE MAN WITH THE BOOK IS SIXTY-FIVE**, not sixty-one, in every present-day reference. He is on the page as sixty-three and
   sixty-five across ch 681–690. **The two historical sixty-ones are other events and did not move.**
3. **`ch 713` says *five years ago*** about his own total, not *in the fourth year*. Neither figure was on the page.
4. **THE FORTY-ONE STROKES TRAVEL IN THE LOCKED DRAWER FROM ch 664**, up and down the tower stair with the four sheets. **They are
   not in the inside of her coat against her ribs and no Volume 12 chapter looks at the margin again.**
5. **THE MAP IS STILL OPEN AND IS NOW PUBLISHED IN THREE NUMBERS.** *How far the rows sit past the wall* has been open since the
   Volume 11 close. `ch 609:47`, `ch 617:187`, `ch 700:11`, `ch 699:13`, `ch 705:15` and the outline put the turn-to-frame leg at
   **thirteen**; `ch 659:5` puts it at **seventeen**; `ch 704:53` puts it at **twenty**. **No phase may pick one of those three to
   suit a sentence. The Batch 0003 review adjudicates it, or the volume close does, and until then no card may state that distance.**

# VOLUME 12 BATCH 0003 CLOSED at ch 705 — what Batch 0004 inherits

**ch 691–705 are on the page, measured and reviewed and repaired. This section turns the four threads the planning fix said this
file owed into threads, and records what the batch closed. The position is `state/current.md` §*Who is where at end of chapter
705*; the figures and the full record are `state/continuity.md` §*Established facts, Volume 12 Batch 0003 — THE FIRSTS* and
§*VOLUME 12 BATCH 0003 — REVIEW REPAIR*.**

## Where she is at the last page, in one line

**Day 2,240, the seventh of the month beginning 2,234, at the ninth hour, at the broken wall, on the east side of a ground of two
hundred people, at IMMORTAL VESSEL 2, six hundred and eighty days lighter, four fingers and a thumb, with a strip in her bag that
has her own total on it twice and fourteen people in that ground who can do the arithmetic she has told nothing.** Twenty-four days
to the seventh first.

## Open, and Batch 0004's engine

1. **THE HUNDRED AND FORTY-YEAR OLD FIGURE UNDER EVERY NAME ON THE SLATE, AND THE FACT THAT IT IS NOT SECRET.** A total is a date,
   a day and a fraction, and the fraction is one in twenty on every ground in this county. **Nobody has to be told how.** Nine
   people were doing it without her by day 2,215 and fourteen by day 2,219 and it is not stopping. **She has told none of them,
   and she will not, and she has three reasons and two of them are good and the third is that she cannot stop counting. This is
   the volume's hardest thing and it is not resolved and Batch 0004 does not resolve it either — it pays it off by ch 718, when
   the levy comes off a board in front of everybody.**
2. **WHAT A FIGURE DOES TO A GROUND, WHICH NOBODY KNOWS AND SHE WILL NOT FIND OUT BY ASKING.** A boy of nineteen told her, as
   arithmetic and not as a warning, that if one of them knew there would be not one of them left on the rows in a month. She has
   carried that since the fifth of the month beginning 2,054 and has not worked out what he was right about and has said so
   out loud. **ch 713 is the brother, and he worked his own out five years ago and told nobody, and that settles nothing.**
3. **Yuan's decision at ch 694.** He has found that a shape is a thing that has to be kept by somebody, that the only person in
   the world who will keep one for him is a woman who has said she cannot do the thing he would have to ask, and he is going to
   ask her anyway. **He cannot say the day and there is a first of a month in thirty days and she does not tell a person things a
   day in advance. He has been in the frame eight times and has never once left it able to do a thing he wanted to do and could
   not do.** **Batch 0004 does not have to stage the asking. It has to make Batch 0004's world one where asking is possible.**
4. **THE OPEN BILL FOR COURT.** Raised out loud at the tenth hour of a night, answered by nobody. **A thing that reads records
   has never wanted one in eleven hundred years and there is one now. She priced it in days and did not say out loud what she
   worked out, which is that it may take the writing off the board and not the person.** Nothing in Batch 0003 said that and
   **Batch 0004 must not say it before ch 718** — ch 718 is its engine.
5. **THE STRIP.** Nine inches of hand-made paper, a square of oilcloth pressed into the middle of it, nothing written on either
   side when it arrived, off the top of the stair, from a cart, from a man who did not ask. **It has her own total on the back
   of it in eighty-one lines and the same sum written underneath in words, and no name on it anywhere, on purpose.** It is the
   first thing that has come to her from above the line and it is not a shape and there was no form she could be given it on.
   **Nobody in this volume has sent her anything else and nobody has told her she was sent one.**
6. **THE FOURTH COLUMN OF THREE HUNDRED AND ONE NAMES, and the fourth one from the bottom.** A name in that column is a name
   that came off that board, and the woman of sixty has been sitting four hundred yards from it for nine years, and Ning Yan is
   not entitled to say any of this to her. **Three hundred and one out of nine hundred and twelve is very nearly a third, and she
   does not know what a third of a name is.** Open, and deliberately.
7. **THE LAMP AT THE FAR END OF THE LONG TABLE.** Lit in nine seconds by something that did not open the door again, and left
   burning on oil a boy of sixteen knows where to keep, in a room that has been shut nine years. **She said out loud: a lamp that
   ought not to be burning is going to be burning on my account, and if a man comes out of that door in nine years he is going to
   think somebody is there.** It is the nearest thing to being met that this world has offered her. **ch 720 puts her in that
   room and the lamp is still hers and still burning.**
8. **THE ABSENCE OF A PLACE.** Immortal Vessel 2's cost, applied: if she is not in the building on the day the carter comes, then
   on the day the carter comes she is nowhere, and there is nothing in this county that joins two records. **ch 716's cost is the
   third and it is *she cannot be counted where she stands*, and the harvest at ch 718 is a count that takes a board and not a
   person, and those two are the same fact arriving twice.**
9. ***Twenty-two days*, still open and still unplaced.** `grep -oi 'twenty-two days'` over `chapters/volume-12/` returns 0 and no
   card has put it in front of her and no card has equated it to a dated interval. **It goes into Volume 13 unspent unless a close
   spends it.**
10. **HOW FAR THE ROWS SIT PAST THE WALL. Still open, still not settled, and now with the fourth statement recorded.** Thirteen
    from the turn to the wall is settled everywhere and is not the open thread; the rows are two or three hundred yards past it
    at ch 618, three at ch 677 and four at ch 659 and ch 660, and **Batch 0003's twenty was withdrawn by its own review because
    it is corroborated nowhere.** **Batch 0004 must state no mile figure on the leg between the rows and the turn. The batch now
    says two days of road, which is its own measurement from ch 700 and settles nothing.**

## Closed and irreversible in Batch 0003, and not to be undone

- **ch 697 — she worked out her own total and said it out loud at the ninth hour in daylight in front of two hundred people, and
  said no first about it in her own head, and said yes and went and did it: four days, one hour and twelve minutes.** A figure
  said out loud where people are is not a figure that can be un-said. **Forty people in that ground can count to eighty. Batch
  0004 does not unsay it and does not write it twice.**
- **The answer to ch 665, given out loud in front of nine people on the eighteenth of the month beginning 2,144: no, you cannot,
  and the question was wrong.** **The asking itself is not retracted, not softened and not asked again.**
- **The fifth term is unenforceable and both of them know it.** A record of what she said cannot tell a true sentence from a false
  one and cannot be checked against a page, and there is no seventh term to fix it. **Batch 0004 may carry this and may not
  re-derive it.**
- **Immortal Vessel 2 taken, at ch 702, day 2,206, and its cost named once.** **Batch 0004 may not name it again.**
- **The four firsts of this band are gone: 2,144 · 2,174 · 2,204 · 2,234, the third, the fourth, the fifth and the sixth.** She was
  at the frame for one of them in nine years and she watched three go by from a distance and from a wall.
- **A stage nobody could come and find.** Taken and applied and not re-derived.

## Still untouched, still unspent, and still Batch 0004's

**The four sheets are in a locked drawer four miles west of a square and the key is in the front of her bag and she did not use
it in thirty days.** **The forty-one strokes have not been looked at and no mouth has referred to them.** **The five blank leaves
are blank and nothing has been put in them, and the batch's irreversible step is a line said out loud where people are and not
written down.** Her mother's name is unsaid. **The ch 658 sentence is not quoted again in Volume 12.** The ch 150 refusal stands
where it stands. The relicensing legs are in order and no card said one was required or not required. No office above the
Standing Office of the Second Account. No harem and nothing a romance. **The lever has now been carried three times in Volume 12
— twice in Batch 0001 and not at all since — and `outline/volume-12.md` allows Volume 12 one more, so Batch 0004's allowance is
spent and it is not pulled.**

## Three things Batch 0004 must not inherit as clean prose

1. **That a number she can work out is hers.** It is not. It is a subtraction and the fraction is one in twenty and there are
   forty people in that ground who can do it and nine of them were already doing it. **Ch 696 says this out loud on the page and
   ch 704 costs her for it, and the sentence may not be argued with.**
2. **That the third column on the slate is a discovery.** It is not. Every man of forty-nine in the county does that column every
   month of his working life and does not know he is doing it. **Ch 701 says so, and the second half of the finding — that the
   figure under her name was right — is the only new thing on the page.**
3. **That a lamp is the same as being met.** She says, in her own words on the page, that it is the nearest thing to it and that
   it is not it, and that she would like it said out loud that she said so on the way down four flights of stairs.
   **Batch 0004 does not soften that and does not promote it.**

## What Batch 0003 must not read as settled, from its own review

**Nineteen defects were repaired and the full record is in `state/continuity.md`. Three of them are about figures a later phase
could otherwise quote:**

- **The batch is 29,240 words of body prose over fifteen chapters at average 1,949** — 29,202 before the repair and 29,212
  after it are both the old page and neither is to be quoted.
- **The date of ch 665's asking is the sixth hour of the twelfth of the month beginning 2,054, day 2,065**, and it was wrong in
  three places including a Batch 0001 chapter, and the Batch 0002 continuity block's line that it was *deliberately* carried
  forward uncorrected no longer describes the page.
- **The rows-to-turn leg has no mile figure on it any more.** Two days of road is the measurement and it is hers.

## What Batch 0004 must not read as settled, from the review OF the review

**The batch's repair was itself reviewed, thirteen further defects were found and fixed, and the full record is at
`state/continuity.md` §*VOLUME 12 BATCH 0003 — REVIEW OF THE REVIEW*. Four of those are things a drafting phase could otherwise
get wrong without noticing:**

- **Two days of road is the rows-to-turn leg and it is the ONLY leg it may be used on.** The first repair had also put it on the
  wall-to-turn leg, which is thirteen miles and settled, and that gave the open map question a second answer. **Thirteen miles is
  the wall to the turn. Two days of road is the rows to the turn. Two miles of flat is the ground to the wall past the gate.**
  No Batch 0004 card may put a mile figure on the rows leg in either direction.
- **A number moved without the sentence around it being re-measured, and it happened twice in this batch.** Every interval in ch 704
  had to be re-derived against a single present day, and every one of the four figures in that chapter that the first pass had
  touched was wrong. **If Batch 0004 finds itself swapping a number into dated prose, the swap is not the work; the re-measurement
  is.**
- **`thank` is 1 across the whole volume, not 0, and that one is a refusal** — *she has not thanked him for that and is not going
  to*, at ch 702. **Batch 0004 may not thank anybody.**
- **Two things were found and deliberately left standing, and both are for the volume close:** `ch 692`'s *the slate has been up
  for eight days*, which cannot be checked without a model of the slate's cycle between the first and the twenty-fourth that this
  batch never wrote down; and the post with a keeper on it, which `ch 601:61` puts nine miles west of the turn and `ch 700` puts
  four miles from it. **Neither is Batch 0004's to arbitrate.**

## VOLUME 12 BATCH 0004 — ch 706–720, *what is written on it*. CLOSED ON THE PAGE AND REPAIRED. A COLD READER STARTS HERE.

Fifteen chapters, **28,680 words of body prose on method B, average 1,913**, day 2,241 to day 2,300. The last batch of Volume 12.
**Volume 12 as a whole: ch 661–720, sixty chapters, 116,754 words of body prose on method B, average 1,946.**

**The repair pass and what it arbitrated.** Fourteen internal contradictions were fixed in place and **no planned beat, dated event or chapter assignment moved.** The dates ch 707, 708, 710, 712, 713, 714, 715 and 718 carried were re-derived from the day number and are now right; **the eighth first stays at day 2,294 and Immortal Vessel 3 stays at day 2,290 on the twenty-seventh of the month beginning 2,264, and the batch's only irreversible step is still the ch 718 harvest.** `outline/volume-12.md` was not edited and the four batches and sixty chapters stand. The figures below and in `state/continuity.md` were all re-run after the repair; **the drafting pass read 28,409 and 116,483 and those are withdrawn.**

### What the batch added

- **The firsts of a month are dates and not events.** What happens at one is that a slate is taken out, wiped, turned over, written in, put back, and carried up a road at the second hour.
